Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [70] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（多体智能体）这一以人为中心的新范式，旨在感知、建模、预测并支持个体人类状态随时间变化的轨迹，将其作为建模、干预和评估的主要对象，而非仅仅完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI存在结构性缺陷：数字智能体仅转换软件状态，具身智能体仅转换物理状态，二者都无法理解用户"忘记、困惑、副作用或故意拒绝"等个人状态，也无法判断何种支持合适，缺乏以人的动态状态和主体性为核心的系统设计。

Method: 提出闭环框架，包含：事件驱动的多模态感知重建个人事件；纵向可纠正记忆提供时间上下文；个人世界模型（Personal World Models）预测替代决策和干预下的未来个人状态与结果；可容许干预策略在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性和用户控制约束下选择相称的支持。系统通过人与环境的反馈持续更新，采用有目的边界、不确定性感知、用户可纠正的表征，而非穷尽式的数字孪生。

Result: 统一了个人助理、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人机系统等分散能力为闭环系统；组织了设计空间（人类状态目标、关系情境、智能体角色）；提出了以场景为中心的评价方法、主体性保持指标、基准需求、边缘原生个人模型及治理方向。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类利益维护，为下一代以人为中心的人工智能系统提供了新的范式框架和设计路线图。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [71] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力战役的AI驱动框架，将整个战役过程作为完整、可追踪的流程进行仿真，支持在受控模拟平台上对多达10万个代理的协调影响进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵威胁已从单一语言模型的诱导性文本扩展到AI群体（swarms），即由协调代理组成的持续群体，它们能适应平台反馈并将有组织的战役伪装成普通社交互动。由于这类战役无法从孤立消息中识别，必须在其规划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量和适应的连续谱系中进行分析。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内呈现完整战役流程，将行动者角色、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化基于模型的评估以及模拟群体中的配置变化连接起来。其架构通过记录行动者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则来支持可复现的研究与红队分析。

Result: 在多达10万个代理的多种配置下进行了评估，结果显示IO Factory能够按规模执行战役时间线，并产生可检查的曝光证据以及配置信念变量的可测量变化。

Conclusion: IO Factory通过记录每次运行中的关键要素，支持可复现的研究和针对协调影响的红队分析，为理解和防御AI驱动的信息操纵提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [72] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，通过在每个推理步骤动态检索并注入已解决的示例到思考轨迹中，解决大型推理模型（LRMs）在延长思维链时出现的收益递减和误差累积问题。在五个推理模型和四个基准测试上，相比标准测试时扩展，准确率持续提升，在AIME 2025上相对增益高达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型依靠增加推理时计算量来提升性能，但研究表明顺序式的测试时扩展往往收益递减甚至为负，因为更长的推理轨迹会带来不确定性增加、错误累积和偏离原始问题。现有方法缺乏在推理过程中提供示例引导的机制。

Method: 提出ThinkRetrieve框架，在LRM推理的每个中间步骤，从外部问题-逐步解答语料库中动态检索相关示例，并直接注入到思考轨迹中，为模型提供"如何推理"的指导而非仅仅是"哪些事实相关"。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ四个基准进行实验，ThinkRetrieve相比标准测试时扩展持续提升准确率，在AIME 2025上相对增益高达60%。

Conclusion: ThinkRetrieve通过动态示例检索注入的方式，有效改善了测试时扩展的收益递减问题，表明在推理过程中提供结构化示例引导比单纯延长推理链更为有效。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [73] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCGR，一个联邦生成式跨域推荐框架，将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID序列，通过共享词表实现跨域对齐，同时通过可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号，并采用原型个性化生成器实现选择性参数聚合，在六个Amazon跨域场景中优于联邦生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域推荐在联邦部署下面临跨域对齐困难，因为行为锚点（如重叠用户和共享交互信号）稀疏、不可用或涉及隐私；直接联邦化SID生成器会面临SID分词器固定导致的语义瓶颈（本地CF信号无法全局共享）以及联邦平均在域异构下引发负迁移的问题。

Method: 提出FedCGR框架，具体包括三个关键设计：(1) 将物品表示为来自公共元数据的离散语义ID序列，通过共享词表而非私有交互诱导跨域对齐；(2) 设计可靠性感知语义接口，将本地CF证据注入生成模型；(3) 训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域专属参数本地化。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域场景的实验结果表明，FedCGR持续优于联邦生成式基线方法，并且在全排序和采样评估协议下与强顺序和联邦CDR方法相比达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 通过稳定的语义物品语言和显式适应性设计，联邦生成式跨域推荐可以同时解决跨域对齐困难和联邦部署约束，为隐私保护下的跨域推荐提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [74] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出XCoT-VLA，用紧凑可执行的XCoT令牌替代冗长的自然语言链式推理，实现驾驶场景中场景理解与轨迹生成的直接连接，在降低推理开销的同时提升轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型依赖冗长的自然语言CoT进行推理，但这种方式开放性强、解码成本高、难以作为面向动作的表示进行优化，不适合实时驾驶控制场景。

Method: 1）从自动构建的Reason-Action监督中学习紧凑可执行的CoT令牌（XCoT）；2）使用确定性令牌函数路由，将Reason FFN应用于XCoT令牌、Control FFN应用于轨迹查询，实现流匹配轨迹生成；3）提出XCoT策略优化（XCPO）作为可选的细化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布数据集上，纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323；在变道场景中，横向FDE从1.616降至0.648；仅用2-6个可执行XCoT令牌即可在实时规划预算内完成推理。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向的推理可以做到紧凑、可执行且与轨迹生成直接相连，XCoT-VLA证明了在保持实时性的同时提升轨迹预测精度的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [75] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，通过可执行计算树从真实表格生成金融推理基准，无需人工标注且答案天然正确。实验发现模型在推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，在对抗性数值扰动下下降高达47%。轻量级LoRA微调可显著提升模型在金融表格推理上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域已取得显著进展，但基于结构化数据的金融推理评估仍相对不足。需要一种可扩展、无标注成本且答案可靠的金融推理基准构建方法。

Method: V-FiLLM框架从真实表格构建可执行计算树，通过符号化评估获得ground truth，再将计算树渲染为自然语言问题，完全绕过模型标注。框架提供四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文规模。

Result: 开源模型在推理深度增加时准确率下降最高达51%，在对抗性数值扰动下下降最高达47%。LoRA微调将准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%（留出问题），并在FinQA上比基础模型高出5个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM提供了一种可扩展、无标注成本的金融推理基准生成框架，同时揭示了现有模型在深度推理和数值鲁棒性方面的不足。针对性、低成本的LoRA微调是提升组合推理能力的有效方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [76] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种无需评估的自进化智能体技能压缩方法，通过将技能结构化为带类型的契约（contract）和残差（residual）两部分，并采用最小描述长度目标进行优化，同时满足所有触发条件、工作流边、工具需求和输出字段的硬覆盖约束。支持一次性压缩和持续的写时压缩两种模式，在压缩率、泛化性和成本方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在累积技能时会产生冗余：相同规则被重复陈述、常见动作序列被复制而非复用，导致技能注入成本高、难以维护。通用的提示压缩方法不适用于技能压缩，因为技能不是平坦的文本段落——其名称和描述决定适用条件，工作流控制执行，工具和输出契约约束有效性，而罕见例外即使未被采样任务激活也可能至关重要。基于评估引导的压缩虽然能测试这些行为，但引入了回放、成本和压缩时评估集的依赖。

Method: SkillZip的核心理念是"一次解释，多次引用"：在适用作用域内将重复规则只陈述一次，将重复动作序列因子化为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式异常。形式上，该方法将技能分解为技能契约和残差，构建带类型的最小描述长度目标函数，并施加硬覆盖约束以确保每个提取的触发器、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段都被覆盖。该方法提供简单的共享阈值，通过构造保留唯一的罕见规则，并支持高效的局部更新。SkillZip有两种模式：一次性模式（一次结构化提取调用加确定性优化）和持续的写时压缩模式（在每次自进化补丁到来时增量整合，无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史）。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销方面均显示出优于现有方法的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip提供了一种无需评估的自进化智能体技能压缩方案，通过结构化解释和最小描述长度优化，在不牺牲技能完整性的前提下实现高效压缩，且支持持续化的增量更新。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [77] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了轨道交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，通过统一模式显式建模城市实体间的空间与语义交互，为城市级轨道交通站点相关任务提供数据支撑，并在站点区域商店推荐和客流预测任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测）需要大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织上往往忽视各类城市实体之间的复杂交互关系，导致数据利用率不高、任务效果受限。

Method: 构建RTSKG知识图谱数据集，集成轨道交通站点、道路路段、兴趣点等多类异构城市实体，采用统一模式（unified schema）显式建模实体间的空间和语义交互，以Linked Data形式公开访问。

Result: 在站点区域商店推荐和知识增强客流预测两项任务上的评估表明，RTSKG有效提升了任务表现，证明了其在支持城市级轨道交通站点分析方面的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为一种结构化的城市数据组织方式，能够显式建模城市实体间的复杂交互关系，有效支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务，为后续研究与实际应用提供了可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [78] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究agentic编程中README（如CLAUDE.md）的无限增长问题，将其命名为"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering），并通过实验证明在提示词中添加注释可以有效阻止这一增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实仓库中的agentic编程README（如CLAUDE.md）会无限制地增长，直到仓库退役或有人整体重写文件。这是因为不完美的回忆机制：追加指令总是便宜的，但一旦指令的理由丢失，删除该指令而不冒正确性回归的风险需要O(2^|D|)的成本。作者将其命名为"灾难性记忆"，与持续学习中的"灾难性遗忘"相对应。

Method: 1. 在1,867个仓库中追踪247,694条指令的生命周期来表征该现象；2. 通过反转IFEval创建可验证的世界，其中最优提示词是已知的，用来检验提示词注释的效果；3. 将相同的反转方法应用于WildIFEval，测试真实世界的agentic指令遵循改进。

Result: 1. agentic提示词无界增长，生命周期内增长超过3倍（+226%），每次提交净增4.9条指令；指令越老越不可能被删除（log-hazard -0.032/commit）。2. 提示词中的注释编码潜在推理可以移除99.3%的多余指令（从+211.3%降至+1.4%）。3. 在WildIFEval上，提示词注释可将真实世界agentic指令遵循提升最多23.1%。

Conclusion: 如果英语是新的代码，为什么还没有注释？提示词注释是阻止agentic提示词无界增长的有效手段，可以在减少冗余指令的同时提升指令遵循性能。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [79] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN，一种将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为一等公民引入逻辑张量网络（LTN）的神经符号扩展框架，使时间和序列约束可以直接在逻辑层面表达，并提供PyTorch实现和开源库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的LTN框架主要适用于扁平化个体集合的数据表示，无法显式捕捉时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构化组织信息，这限制了其在时序和关系推理任务中的应用。

Method: sLTN将结构维度作为命名张量轴引入语言，使其可以被显式量化、通过结构关系关联，并用于在逻辑层面直接表达时间、序列和关系约束。作者形式化了sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义，证明了无结构维度时sLTN退化为原始LTN，并基于PyTorch实现了声明式签名、公式解析和张量化解释。

Result: 该框架在代表性的时间和序列推理示例上进行了展示验证，并提供了开源实现（sltn库，托管于GitHub）。

Conclusion: sLTN扩展了LTN的能力边界，使神经符号推理能够自然地处理结构化数据，同时保持与原始LTN的兼容性（作为特例），为时序和序列建模中的逻辑约束集成提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [How to Dogfood Your AI Chat Agent: A Three-Layer Evaluation Framework with Goal-Directed NPC Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09939)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三层"dogfooding"评估框架，用于系统性验证真实用户能否通过多轮对话实现目标。框架结合了标准问题库测试、随机游走多轮评估和基于目标导向NPC模拟器的测试，在为期三个月的生产环境案例研究中验证了其有效性，NPC模拟器以0.17美元/次的成本实现了77%的目标达成率，能够支撑每日CI/CD集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM聊天智能体的生产团队面临质量保障空白：现有评估工具要么测试单个响应，要么模拟社交互动，但没有工具能系统性验证真实用户能否通过多轮对话实现目标。这一缺口导致无法确保生产环境中智能体真正帮助用户达成目的。

Method: 提出三层dogfooding框架：第1层为规范问题库测试（canonical question-bank testing）；第2层为随机游走多轮评估（random-walk multi-turn evaluation）；第3层为目标导向NPC模拟器，包含五种结构化目标类型和十大类失败分类体系。在包含257次评估运行和108个场景NPC套件的生产多智能体系统上进行为期三个月的纵向案例研究。

Result: 三层产生互补的回归信号：同步运行中跨层响应质量相关性弱（Spearman rho在-0.15到0.14之间），纵向序列中呈负相关（rho低至-0.46），证实规范正确性不能预测目标导向对话成功。NPC模拟器以0.17美元/次成本实现77%目标达成率，比人工评估便宜6272倍，实现每日CI/CD集成及自动化的PROMOTE/HOLD/ROLLBACK发布决策。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥补了LLM聊天智能体质量评估的空白，三层方法产生了互补而非冗余的回归信号。框架以极低成本实现了高频自动化评估，并提供了完整提示模板、失败分类体系和Python可复现指南，便于其他团队采用。

Abstract: Production teams deploying LLM chat agents face a specific quality assurance gap: existing evaluation tools test individual responses or simulate social interactions, but none systematically verify whether real users can achieve their goals through multi-turn conversation. We introduce a three-layer dogfooding framework that bridges this gap by combining canonical question-bank testing (Layer 1), random-walk multi-turn evaluation (Layer 2), and a goal-directed NPC (Non-Player Character) simulator with five structured goal types and a ten-category failure taxonomy (Layer 3). In a longitudinal case study on a production multi-agent system over roughly three months (257 evaluation runs; a 108-scenario NPC suite), we find that the three layers produce complementary regression signals: cross-layer correlation for response quality is weak within a synchronized run (Spearman rho between -0.15 and 0.14) and negative across the longitudinal series (rho down to -0.46), confirming that canonical correctness does not predict goal-directed conversation success. The NPC simulator achieves 77 percent goal achievement at 0.17 dollars per run (6,272x cheaper than human evaluation), enabling daily CI/CD integration with automated PROMOTE/HOLD/ROLLBACK release decisions. We release full prompt templates, the failure taxonomy, and a Python-first replicability guide so that other teams can adopt the framework for their own LLM chat agents.

</details>


### [81] [The impact of design factors of virtual and augmented reality on tertiary students user experience in Metaverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09940)
*Julius G. Garcia,Alvie Simonette Q. Alip*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究调查了VR和AR元宇宙平台中视觉元素、环境设计和通信功能对用户体验（UX）的影响。通过对321名高校学生的数据进行分析，发现环境设计（β=0.424）和视觉元素（β=0.298）是用户体验的主要预测因素，共同解释47.3%的方差；而通信功能对用户体验无显著影响（β=0.053, p=0.196）。研究建议开发者和教育机构优先考虑环境设计以提升沉浸式学习体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元宇宙在教育领域的应用增长迅速，但视觉元素、环境设计和通信功能等具体设计因素对用户体验的具体贡献尚不明确，这限制了基于证据的设计决策和资源分配优化。

Method: 采用相关研究设计，从四所高等教育机构中通过目的性抽样选取321名熟悉元宇宙平台的工程与计算机科学专业学生。使用经过验证的5点李克特量表结构化问卷测量用户体验及三类设计因素，通过多元回归分析评估各因素对用户体验的独立和联合效应。

Result: 环境设计（β=0.424, p<0.001）和视觉元素（β=0.298, p<0.001）是用户体验的主要预测因素，两者联合解释47.3%的方差，且二者之间存在强相关（r=0.792）。通信功能对用户体验的影响不显著（β=0.053, p=0.196）。

Conclusion: 设计良好、互动性强的虚拟环境对元宇宙用户体验至关重要。研究建议开发者和教育机构应优先关注环境设计以推动沉浸式学习及更广泛的元宇宙应用，同时通信功能设计需更好地支持社交互动。

Abstract: The Metaverse is a convergent space integrating virtual reality (VR) and augmented reality (AR) technologies, with market projections rising from \$65.5 billion in 2022 to \$1.3 trillion by 2030. Despite rapid adoption in education, the specific contributions of visual elements, environmental design, and communication features to user experience (UX) remain underexplored, limiting evidence-based design and resource allocation. This study examined how these design factors in VR and AR environments influence UX in Metaverse platforms. Using a correlational research design, data were collected from 321 purposively sampled tertiary students from engineering and computer science departments across four higher education institutions, all familiar with Metaverse platforms. A structured questionnaire with validated 5-point Likert scales measured UX; visual elements (field of view, resolution, color, complexity, and style); environmental design (interactivity, cohesiveness, naturalness, and complexity); and communication features (text/audio/video tools, avatar interaction, and spatial audio). Multiple regression analysis assessed the independent and combined effects of these factors on UX. Results show that environmental design (b=0.424, p<0.001) and visual elements (b=0.298, p<0.001) are the primary predictors of UX, jointly explaining 47.3% of its variance, with a strong correlation between them (r=0.792). Communication features did not significantly influence UX (b=0.053, p=0.196), challenging assumptions about virtual connectedness and indicating that communication design should better support social interaction. These findings highlight the importance of well-designed, interactive virtual spaces and suggest that developers and educational institutions prioritize environmental design to advance immersive learning and broader Metaverse applications.

</details>


### [82] [EweAcT: Ewe behaviour aligned to accelerometer data for activity monitoring in extensive grazing systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09943)
*Lucile Riaboff,Ny Aina Andriamampandry,Jean-François Bompa,Mathias Aletru,Christian Durand,Sébastien Douls,Gaëtan Bonnafe,Morgane Costes-Thiré,Guillaume Delosières,Jean- Marc Mongrelet,Enzo Niro,Némuel Tadi,Séverine Deretz,Sara Parisot,Margot Lamarque,Dominique Hazard,Emilie Cobo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于监测放牧绵羊行为的加速度计数据集。数据集包含120只Romane母羊（2021-2024年出生）的79小时三轴加速度计数据，与人工标注的5种主要行为（采食、反刍、休息、移动、其他）对齐。数据来自法国INRAE的La Fage实验站广泛放牧条件下采集，涵盖斜坡牧场和热浪期等多样条件，可用于训练AI模型进行行为分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广泛放牧条件下监测牲畜行为可评估动物对环境扰动（如热浪、寄生虫、捕食者攻击）的适应能力。加速度计数据结合AI模型可实现行为监测，但需要大量与标注行为对齐的数据来开发可靠的预测模型，尤其需要覆盖多种代表性放牧条件的数据。

Method: 研究分两批采集数据：第一批于2024年3月、6月和7月在UEF采集，涵盖斜坡牧草和热浪期等广泛放牧条件，绵羊佩戴特制加速度计项圈，通过高空CCTV摄像头视频录制并使用BORIS软件人工标注行为，用Python实现时间同步对齐；第二批于2025年11月补充采集移动行为数据，通过绵羊从圈舍到牧场的约10分钟步行路径采集移动数据并合并。

Result: 最终数据集包含79小时三轴加速度计数据，覆盖120只来自不同遗传品系（社会吸引力高低和人类耐受性高低）的Romane母羊，包含5种主要放牧行为（采食、反刍、休息、移动、其他）的标注数据，可直接用于训练AI模型进行行为分类。

Conclusion: 该数据集可用于应用人工智能模型对广泛放牧系统中绵羊的5种主要行为进行加速度计数据分类，为后续行为预测模型的开发提供了覆盖多种放牧条件的高质量标注数据基础。

Abstract: Monitoring livestock behaviour under extensive conditions would provide valuable insights to assess animal adaption to environmental perturbations in agroecological systems (e.g., heat waves, parasitism, predator attacks). Animal behaviour can be monitored using accelerometer data collected from neck-collars combined with artificial intelligence models. However, large amounts of accelerometer data aligned with annotated behaviours are necessary to develop accurate models of behaviour prediction. In particular, developing reliable models for extensive systems requires data collected across a wide range of representative conditions. The dataset includes 79 hours of tri-axial accelerometer data aligned with behaviours manually annotated from video recordings for 120 Romane ewes born between 2021 and 2024. The ewes were derived from two divergent genetic lines after three and four generations of selection started 10 years ago: low and high social attractiveness, noted S-and S+, and low and high tolerance towards humans, noted H-and H+. They were reared under the extensive system applied to the Experimental Unit of La Fage (UEF, INRAE, Saint-Jean-et-saint Paul, Aveyron) where 250 sheep were reared exclusively outdoors on 280 hectares of rangeland in southern France. First batch of data was collected on March, June and July 2024 at the UEF under a range of extensive conditions, including sloping pastures and heat-wave periods. Ewes were equipped with accelerometer neck-collars specifically designed for young sheep on pasture. They were grouped on experimental paddocks for 4 to 8 hours and provided with fresh grass and ad libitum access to water. The animals were simultaneously video-recorded using an elevated CCTV camera. Behaviour annotation was carried out using Behavioral Observation Research Interactive Software focusing on the main behaviours on pasture: Grazing, Ruminating, Resting, Moving, and ''Other'', grouping all remaining activities. Annotations and corresponding accelerometer sequences were aligned using Python language, based on a time synchronization procedure. A second batch of data was acquired on November 2025 to supplement the dataset with the moving activity. For that purpose, ewes were equipped with the accelerometer collars and moved on tracks from the housing area to the pastures, corresponding to an approximately 10 minute-walk. The start and end times of the moves for each ewe were used to align the corresponding accelerometer data with the moving activity. These data were then merged with the dataset from the first batch. The resulting dataset is ready to use for applying artificial intelligence models to classify the 5 main behaviours of sheep under extensive grazing systems from accelerometer data.

</details>


### [83] [Navigation Alone Is Not Enough: Evaluating Explanatory Assistive UI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09944)
*Santosh Patapati*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NeXUI，一个面向辅助智能体的基准测试，要求智能体在导航网页界面的同时以清晰的语言解释每一步操作，以支持盲人和低视力用户。该基准从安全性、效率、任务成功率及解释的界面依据性等多维度评估智能体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网页界面在动态更新或依赖视觉布局时对屏幕阅读器用户造成巨大障碍。现有的UI智能体虽然能操作界面，但缺乏对用户的解释和让用户保持控制的协作能力，而现有基准主要关注任务完成率，未评估智能体的解释能力和用户监督支持。

Method: NeXUI将真实的用户目标与仪器化的界面状态配对，使智能体能够同时从视觉上下文和结构信息中推理。评估体系同时衡量安全性、效率、任务成功率，并检查操作解释是否基于界面实际状态。

Result: 即使是最先进的Gemini-3.5-Flash模型，在NeXUI上也仅有44%的成功率和较差的解释得分，表明该基准对现有模型具有显著挑战性。

Conclusion: NeXUI通过聚焦导航、解释和用户控制三个方面，为研究能够支持盲人和低视力用户在现代化计算环境中的辅助智能体提供了更清晰的评估框架和未来研究的基础。

Abstract: Modern web interfaces are increasingly difficult to use with screen readers, particularly when pages update dynamically or hide important structure behind visual layout. Recent UI agents can act on such interfaces; however, for assistive agents to be truly useful, they must behave as collaborators that keep users informed and in control, rather than as tools that simply take actions on users' behalf. Most existing benchmarks judge systems primarily by task completion, without assessing how well they explain their actions or support user oversight. We introduce NeXUI, a benchmark for assistive agents that must navigate interfaces while explaining each step in clear language for nonvisual use. NeXUI pairs realistic user goals with instrumented interface states, enabling agents to reason from both visual context and structural information. Its evaluation measures safety, efficiency, and task success, while also checking whether explanations are grounded in the interface state. In our experiments, we find that NeXUI remains challenging even for state-of-the-art foundation models, with % Gemini-3.5-Flash achieving only a 44\% success rate and poor explanation scores, making it a useful foundation for future research and development. By focusing on navigation, explanation, and user control, NeXUI provides a clearer way to study agents that can support blind and visually impaired users in modern computing environments.

</details>


### [84] [Co-Lecturing With the DED: Explaining Circuit Design via the Draw Encode Display Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09945)
*Alasdair Lambert,Guillaume Allais,Conor Mc Bride*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Draw-Encode-Display (DED)循环教学法和配套硬件描述语言Syrup，帮助学生将二维手绘电路图与可测试的数字电路代码联系起来，并通过两届学生调查验证该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维手绘电路图虽直观但难以精确定义和测试，而硬件描述语言精确可测试却缺乏直观性，学生在两者之间转换时存在困难，需要一种系统化的方法来弥补这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出DED（Draw-Encode-Display）循环式教学法：先通过真值表等可视化中间表示绘制电路图；再通过标注输入、输出和中间值将电路图直接转换为代码；最后用内部图解渲染器显示代码结果。同时开发了教育导向的硬件描述语言Syrup支持这一流程。

Result: 通过两届学生群体的调查数据支持了该方法，表明DED循环和Syrup语言能够有效帮助学生完成从自然语言描述到可测试电路代码的转换。

Conclusion: DED循环教学法和Syrup硬件描述语言成功架起了直观手绘电路图与精确定义可测试电路代码之间的桥梁，为数字电路教学提供了系统性方法。

Abstract: When representing digital circuits, 2 dimensional hand drawings free us from the linear structure of hardware description languages, enabling intuitive reasoning and making structure explicit. However these drawings are imprecise and inert: they do not enforce that the circuits are well defined and cannot be tested. We want both intuitive visual representations and well defined testable ones but students can struggle to link one to the other.
  To bridge this gap we present the Draw Encode Display Loop (DED), a Co-Lecturing dynamic which equips students with a systematic method to tackle natural language specifications:
  1. Draw: visually informative intermediate representations (truth tables, characteristic tables) to generate a structured circuit diagram.
  2. Encode: the diagram by labelling inputs, outputs and intermediate values which can be directly converted to code.
  3. Display: the code using an in-house diagrammatic renderer.
  This is supported by Syrup, an education-focused hardware description language which allows students to define, experiment with and display their own circuits. We support these approaches with survey data gathered from two cohorts of students.

</details>


### [85] [HoosierHelp: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Social Service Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09946)
*Yiyang Li,Weixiang Sun,Tianyi Ma,Kaiwen Shi,Zheyuan Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出HoosierHelp，一个基于3,971个印第安纳州公共服务资源的交互式基准，用于评估LLM智能体在社交服务导航中的表现。实验表明当前LLM智能体在该任务上可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交服务导航需要将求助者与满足其需求和约束的资源进行连接，LLM智能体虽有潜力，但现有基准未能捕捉该场景中交互复杂性和约束落地（constraint-grounding）的需求。

Method: 构建HoosierHelp交互式基准，基于3,971个公共服务资源；智能体与模拟用户交互、发起结构化资源搜索调用、处理非理想交互并选择最终资源返回。模拟用户通过变化需求结构、约束可满足性和行为模式（不耐烦、冗长、无依据请求、自我矛盾）来增强真实感。

Result: 在240个样本上对7个LLM进行实验，显示当前LLM智能体在社交服务导航上表现显著不可靠；在需要备用方案（fallback）和自我矛盾的对话中性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 需要开发对复杂和非理想用户交互更加鲁棒的智能体，以提升社交服务导航的可靠性。

Abstract: Social service navigation requires connecting help-seeking individuals to resources that satisfy their needs and specific constraints. Although LLM agents offer a promising interface for conversational resource navigation, existing benchmarks do not capture the interaction complexity and constraint-grounding demands of this setting. We introduce HoosierHelp, an interactive benchmark grounded in 3,971 Indiana public social service resources. Agents interact with simulated users, issue structured resource-search calls, handle non-ideal interactions, and select the final resources returned by the tool. HoosierHelp enhances the realism of simulated users by varying their need structure, constraint satisfiability, and behavior patterns, including impatience, rambling, unsupported requests, and self-contradiction. Experiments on 240 samples across seven LLMs show that current LLM agents remain substantially unreliable for social service navigation. Performance drops sharply on fallback-required and self-contradictory conversations, highlighting the need for agents that are more robust to complex and non-ideal user interactions.

</details>


### [86] [Mapping Multimodal Pilot Stress and Fatigue During Flight Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09947)
*Atandrila Chowdhury,Sudip Vhaduri,Julius Keller,Debra Henneberry,Mark Wilson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究结合生理与自我报告测量，分析了学生飞行员在飞行训练中压力和疲劳的模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行训练中的压力和疲劳对学生飞行员的健康和安全有重要影响，但此前缺乏结合生理信号与主观报告的综合分析。

Method: 使用PSS-10量表在每次飞行前后测量自我报告的压力和疲劳，同时在飞行中连续记录心率、皮电活动、皮肤温度和加速度等生理数据，并跨飞行阶段进行信号预处理和分析。

Result: 发现随飞行次数增加，皮电活动和皮肤温度持续上升，显示工作负荷相关疲劳的累积；飞行后自我报告的疲劳感显著增加；心率反应更具事件特异性，仅在飞行高需求阶段出现短暂峰值。

Conclusion: 结合生理信号与主观报告能有效识别真实飞行训练中的压力和疲劳模式，数据驱动方法有望用于监测飞行员身心健康。

Abstract: This study analyzes patterns of stress and exhaustion among student pilots throughout flight training using a combination of physiological and self-reported measurements. The Perceived Stress Scale (PSS-10) was used to measure perceived stress and exhaustion before and after each flight, while physiological data, including heart rate (HR), electrodermal activity (EDA), skin temperature, and acceleration, were continuously recorded during flight sessions. To identify recurring patterns in arousal and workload, physiological signals were preprocessed and analyzed across the flight stages. The findings indicate a buildup of workload-related weariness over time, as evidenced by steady increases in EDA and skin temperature across flights, as well as post-flight increases in self-reported exhaustion. Heart rate responses were more event-specific, with brief spikes during high-demand phases of flight. Overall, the findings demonstrate the value of combining physiological signals with subjective reports to identify patterns of stress and fatigue during real-world flight training and highlight the potential of data-driven approaches for monitoring pilot well-being.

</details>


### [87] [Immersive Micromanipulation Integrating Pipette and Injector Operations with McKibben-Based Haptic Sensations for Workload Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10033)
*Kenta Yokoe,Sumiwa Saito,Yuki Funabora,Tomoko Isomura,Tadayoshi Aoyama*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种沉浸式显微操作系统，通过McKibben气动人工肌肉提供触觉反馈，统一了ICSI显微操作中的移液管和注射器操作界面，实验证明该系统提高了操作速度并降低了认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的ICSI显微操作依赖视觉反馈，且需要在移液管移动和注射器操作之间频繁切换界面。现有触觉反馈系统仅关注穿刺力，缺乏对注射器状态的反馈，导致操作员认知负荷高、操作效率低。

Method: 开发了一个沉浸式显微操作系统，统一操作接口，利用McKibben气动人工肌肉产生触觉反馈，模拟吸液、排液以及卵母细胞与移液管之间的接触。用户可用单手同时操作移液管和注射器，并在操作过程中接收触觉反馈。通过人体参与实验评估系统性能。

Result: 人体实验表明，沉浸式操作界面相比传统方法提高了显微操作速度，并降低了认知负荷；McKibben触觉反馈提升了系统的整体可用性。

Conclusion: 集成McKibben触觉反馈的沉浸式显微操作系统成功统一了操作接口，降低了操作员的工作负荷，为ICSI显微操作提供了更高效直观的交互方式。

Abstract: Intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) requires advanced micromanipulation techniques but relies solely on visual feedback and involves frequent interface switching between pipette movement and injector operations. Existing haptic feedback systems primarily focus on pipette puncture forces and do not provide feedback on injector states. We developed an immersive micromanipulation system that unifies operational interfaces and provides McKibben-based haptic sensations to represent aspiration, discharge, and contact between the oocyte and pipette. Users operated both the pipette and injector with a single hand while receiving haptic sensations. A human-participant experiment revealed that the immersive operation interface improved micromanipulation speed and reduced cognitive workload of the micromanipulation compared with conventional methods. Additionally, McKibben-based haptic sensations improved overall system usability. The immersive micromanipulation system with McKibben-based haptic sensations successfully unified operational interfaces and reduced operator workload.

</details>


### [88] [Outer Limits: An Experimental Approach to Controlled Content Manipulation within the Reddit Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10115)
*Chenchen Mao,Hanjing Shi,Haiyan Jia,Daniel Unhuryan,Eric Baumer,Dominic DiFranzo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Outer Limits，一个基于浏览器的可控内容实验系统，运行在真实Old Reddit界面中而非模拟环境中。该系统允许独立研究人员在现有社交平台内进行精准控制的实验，同时将实验内容和写入互动与Reddit宿主社区隔离。通过219名参与者的感知保真度研究验证了系统有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立研究人员通常缺乏在真实社交媒体平台上进行受控实验的干预能力，现有方案要么使用重建模拟环境（缺乏真实平台真实感），要么直接在平台操作（无法控制内容和隔离实验影响），因此需要一种兼顾精准控制、真实界面和内容隔离的系统方案。

Method: 开发了基于浏览器的Outer Limits系统：在旧版Reddit界面中本地渲染实验内容、记录研究事件，并配置投票和评论操作，使构造内容及实验性写入互动均不会到达Reddit服务器。通过219名参与者的感知保真度研究（ART ANOVA分析）评估系统，并辅以TOST等价性检验。

Result: 感知保真度研究中，ART ANOVA未发现帖子类型、参与者知晓情况或交互效应的显著差异；探索性TOST检验在d=±0.50等价标准下，边际对比及预告子组的帖子类型对比均满足等价性。此外还通过一个因子设计研究（变化帖子框架、评论框架和评论立场）展示了系统的应用。

Conclusion: Outer Limits独特地结合了三种此前方案各自单独具备的特性：对实验内容的精准控制、现有平台界面、以及将实验内容和互动与宿主社区隔离。该系统为独立研究人员提供了在真实社交平台界面上进行受控实验的可行途径。

Abstract: Independent researchers often lack access to intervention capabilities for controlled experiments on live social media platforms. We present Outer Limits, a browser-based system for controlled content experiments within the existing Old Reddit interface, rather than in a reconstructed simulation. The system renders content locally, records study events, and contains configured voting and commenting actions so that neither constructed content nor experimental write interactions reach Reddit. In a 219-participant perceptual-fidelity study, ART ANOVAs found no significant Post Type, Participant Awareness, or interaction effects. Exploratory TOSTs met the d = plus-minus 0.50 equivalence criterion for the marginal contrasts and for Post Type within the forewarned subgroup. We also illustrate the system with a factorial study varying post frame, comment frame, and comment stance. Outer Limits combines three properties that the approaches considered here provide separately: precise control over experimental content, an existing platform interface, and containment of experimental content and interactions from the host community.

</details>


### [89] [Predicting affective connotation of visualizations from their constituent colors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10169)
*Karen B. Schloss,Halle C. Braun,Kushin Mukherjee,Anna L. Chinni,Seth R. Gorelik*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究验证了能否通过聚合单个颜色的情感联想来预测整个可视化的情感内涵。实验发现，当颜色在色标中均匀分布时，个体颜色情感联想的平均值能有效预测整体情感（加性假设）；当数据分布偏向色标一端时，考虑颜色出现频率的加权平均值预测效果更好（数据依赖假设）。该效应在点图和柱状图中也得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感内涵（affective connotation）是视觉传达的重要方面，但目前缺乏预测可视化整体情感内涵的方法。颜色等多个设计属性都会影响情感联想，关键问题在于这些属性如何组合决定整个可视化的情感关联。

Method: 通过三个实验验证：(1) 实验1测试颜色均匀分布的色标图中，个体颜色情感联想均值能否预测整体情感（加性假设）；(2) 实验2测试数据偏向色标一端时，考虑颜色频率的加权均值预测效果是否更好（数据依赖假设）；(3) 实验3检验加性和数据依赖效应能否推广到点图和柱状图。

Result: 实验1支持加性假设，颜色均匀分布时均值预测有效；实验2支持数据依赖假设，数据有偏时加权均值预测更佳；实验3表明上述效应可推广至点图和柱状图。

Conclusion: 从可视化的个体设计组件预测整体情感内涵是可行的，这对自动化情感可视化设计以支持视觉传达具有重要意义。

Abstract: With increasing evidence that affective connotation (emotional association) is an important aspect of visual communication, there is a need for methods to predict affective connotation of visualizations. Many aspects of visualization design, including colors, textures, and shapes, can contribute to affective connotation, and a key question is how multiple design properties combine to determine the emotion association of a whole visualization. In this study, we focused specifically on color and tested whether it is possible to predict the affective connotation of whole visualizations by aggregating the emotion associations of the individual, constituent colors (additivity hypothesis). We also tested whether accounting for the size of colored regions, as determined by the underlying dataset, improved predictions (data-dependence hypothesis). We found that for colormap data visualizations in which colors were well-distributed across all colors in the color scale, the mean estimated associations of individual colors effectively predicted emotional associations of the maps as a whole (additivity; Exp. 1). For colormaps whose underlying datasets were biased to map more to colors at one end of the color scale, emotional associations were better predicted by a weighted mean that accounted for color frequency in the colormap (data-dependence; Exp. 2). Effects of additivity and data-dependence generalized to dot plots and bar charts (Exp. 3). These results suggest it is viable to predict affective connotation of whole visualizations from their individual design components, which has important implications for automating affective visualization design to support visual communication.

</details>


### [90] [Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Codebook-Guided Coding of Students' Mathematics Metaphor Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10276)
*Liang Zhang,Stephen Hwang,Yue Ma,Jinfa Cai*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于LoRA的监督微调是否能提升大语言模型在学生数学隐喻编码任务上的表现。使用2,265条人类编码的6-8年级学生回复语料，比较了GPT-4o mini和GPT-5 mini（仅提示词）与DeepSeek-R1 1.5B和Mistral 7B（微调前后）在效价-强度编码和主题编码两个任务上的表现。结果显示微调显著提升了开源轻量模型的表现和运行间可靠性，使其能与专有提示词模型竞争甚至超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生对数学的隐喻能揭示其态度、信念、身份和体验，但人工专家编码这些主题复杂、语义丰富的开放式回复既耗时又难以规模化，亟需可扩展且可靠的自动化编码方法。

Method: 使用2,265条人类编码的学生回复语料，采用LoRA监督微调技术对两个开源轻量模型（DeepSeek-R1 1.5B和Mistral 7B）进行微调，并在效价-强度编码和主题编码两个任务上，与仅使用提示词的两个专有模型（GPT-4o mini和GPT-5 mini）进行比较。

Result: 微调显著提升了开源轻量模型在两个编码任务上的性能和运行间可靠性；微调后的紧凑开源模型在与专有提示词模型的比较中表现出竞争力，且多数情况下表现更优。

Conclusion: 紧凑型开源LLM能够支持可扩展、本地可控且注重隐私的AI辅助学生隐喻回复测量，为数学教育研究提供了可行的自动化编码方案。

Abstract: Student-generated metaphors about mathematics can reveal students' attitudes, beliefs, identities, and experiences, but human expert coding of these thematically and semantically complex open-ended responses is time-intensive and difficult to scale. This study examines whether LoRA-based supervised fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) can improve their performance on codebook-guided coding tasks for student mathematics metaphors. We used a human-coded corpus of 2,265 Grade 6-8 responses to food- and animal-based metaphor prompts and instructed the LLMs to perform two coding tasks: valence-intensity coding to capture the direction and strength of students' affective orientations toward mathematics, and thematic coding to capture students' framings of mathematics as expressed through their metaphors. We compared two proprietary models, GPT-4o mini and GPT-5 mini, under prompt-only conditions with two open-weight models, DeepSeek-R1 1.5B and Mistral 7B, evaluated before and after fine-tuning. Results show that fine-tuning substantially improved the performance and run-to-run reliability of the open-weight models across both tasks relative to their base versions. The fine-tuned compact open-weight models became competitive with, and often outperformed, the proprietary prompt-only models. These findings suggest that compact open-weight LLMs can support scalable, locally controllable, and privacy-conscious AI-assisted measurement of students' metaphor responses in mathematics education.

</details>


### [91] [Divided Attention Amplifies the Importance of Expectation-Aligned Visualization Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10320)
*Jiho Kim,Anna L. Chinni,Karen B. Schloss,Michael Gleicher*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过两个实验（无限观看时间和有限观看时间）比较了单任务与双任务条件下用户对可视化图表的理解，发现分心注意力会放大期望一致与期望不一致设计之间的性能差距，并引入线性弹道累积器（LBA）框架来建模用户的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅关注可视化解释作为用户唯一任务时的设计可解释性，但现实场景中用户常在多任务环境中使用可视化（如同时监控告警或回复消息），分心注意力可能改变用户对可视化的理解方式，使符合用户期望的设计变得更加重要，这一假设此前未得到系统验证。

Method: 通过两个受控实验（实验一为无限观看时间，实验二为有限观看时间），在单任务与双任务条件下比较颜色、空间位置与语义概念之间的映射（期望一致vs期望违背）对可视化解释的影响；并使用线性弹道累积器（LBA）框架对用户的决策过程进行建模分析。

Result: 分心注意力显著放大了期望一致设计与期望违背设计之间的性能差距，体现在响应时间、解释准确性和时间约束下做出判断的能力三个方面。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在分心注意力条件下使可视化设计与用户期望保持一致的重要性，并提出了一种面向过程的建模方法，用于理解期望和多任务如何共同塑造可视化解释。

Abstract: Studies have shown that visualization design affects interpretability when visualization interpretation is the user's sole task. However, in real-world settings, users often engage with visualizations while performing concurrent tasks, such as when users simultaneously monitor alerts or respond to messages. Such divided attention may alter how users interpret visualizations, potentially increasing the importance of designs that align with viewer expectations. We investigated this possibility through two experiments comparing visualization interpretation under single-task and dual-task conditions. Specifically, we examined how well-established inferred mappings between color, spatial position, and semantic concepts affect interpretation when users perform a concurrent task, both with unlimited viewing time (Exp. 1) and under limited viewing time (Exp. 2). Our results show that divided attention amplifies the performance gap between expectation-aligned and expectation-violating designs, affecting response time, interpretation accuracy, and the ability to produce a judgment under time constraints. To explain these results, we model the user's decision-making process using a Linear Ballistic Accumulator (LBA) framework. Our findings highlight the increased importance of aligning visualization designs with viewer expectations under divided attention and introduce a process-oriented modeling approach to understanding how expectation and multitasking shape visualization interpretation.

</details>


### [92] [Narrative Keyframing for Generative Creative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10337)
*Chao Zhang,Abe Davis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"叙事关键帧"（narrative keyframing）交互技术，借鉴动画中的关键帧概念，让作家在故事特定时刻指定不同类型的叙事约束，再由AI生成中间散文。通过三类关键帧（情节、角色、视角）和用户研究，证明了该方法在AI辅助创意写作中更具可控性、透明性和参与感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI写作工具虽然能提供高层次的条件控制（如情节大纲），但缺乏可定制、迭代和细粒度的控制方式，作家难以精确表达在故事特定时刻的创作意图。

Method: 引入叙事关键帧交互技术，包含三类关键帧：情节关键帧（定义故事重大事件）、角色关键帧（表示角色随叙事的转变）、视角关键帧（通过第一人称叙事捕捉角色对事件的体验）。系统采用动画关键帧的灵感，将故事规划与生成文本的自适应控制相连接，并通过用户研究评估。

Result: 用户研究结果显示，叙事关键帧支持了更可控、更透明且更具参与感的生成式AI创意写作体验，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 叙事关键帧为AI辅助创意写作提供了一种灵活的新范式，尤其通过视角关键帧引入了对角色塑造和聚焦（focalization）的新控制维度，是对现有AI写作工具的一次有价值的扩展。

Abstract: We introduce narrative keyframing, an interaction technique for AI-assisted creative writing that lets writers specify different types of narrative constraints at selected moments in a story, then use AI to generate intervening prose. Inspired by the use of keyframing in animation, narrative keyframing offers a flexible way to connect story planning with adaptive control over generated text. We explore three types of keyframes: plot keyframes define significant events in a story, character keyframes represent how individual characters change over the narrative, and perspective keyframes capture how individual characters experience different events through first-person narratives. Plot and character keyframes offer a flexible way to adapt the type of high-level conditioning explored in previous AI writing tools to more customizable, iterative, and fine-scale control, while perspective keyframes add a new way to control characterization and focalization by using first-person narratives as an intermediary. Through a user study, we show that narrative keyframing supports a more controllable, transparent, and engaging way to use generative AI in creative writing.

</details>


### [93] [ResonaVis: Visualizing Interactive Music Data to Support Reflective Music Composition for Therapeutic Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10338)
*Abhishek Karwankar,Elise Ruggiero,Daniel Stevens,Matthew Louis Mauriello*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ResonaVis，一个交互式可视化系统，帮助作曲家分析自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）儿童在音乐治疗中的互动与音频数据，从而支持数据驱动的音乐创作反思。混合方法评估显示系统具有良好的可用性（SUS=72.23），显著提升了作曲家在ASD音乐创作中解读互动与声学数据的信心（p<0.05）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在治疗性音乐创作中，作曲家需要在音乐结构与听众感官反应之间导航复杂关系，但缺乏结构化的交互表征，往往依赖直觉。本研究旨在为作曲家提供数据驱动的分析工具，以弥补这一空白。

Method: 开发了ResonaVis可视化系统，整合音频特征与互动日志，通过时间转移、层共现、节奏活动和频谱特性等协调可视化来捕捉儿童与分层音乐作品的互动模式。采用混合方法评估：8名音乐学生的研究加上2名经验丰富作曲家的后续案例研究。

Result: 系统SUS得分72.23，超过早期原型基准。参与者能识别互动模式、推理层间关系并做出知情的创作决策，感知表现高且挫败感低。在ASD聚焦创作中解读互动与声学数据的信心在多个维度显著提升（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了治疗性音乐互动数据的可视化设计空间、支持创作反思的集成系统，以及可视化工具支持分析推理和信心提升的实证证据，表明作曲家正转向更具适应性和数据驱动的创作实践。

Abstract: Designing music for therapeutic contexts requires navigating complex relationships between musical structure and listeners' sensory responses, yet composers often lack structured representations of these interactions, relying instead on intuition. We present ResonaVis, an interactive visualization system that helps composers analyze interaction and audio data from prior sessions with children with Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD), informing future compositions. ResonaVis integrates audio features and interaction logs to capture how children engage with layered musical compositions, representing this engagement through coordinated visualizations of temporal transitions, layer co-occurrence, rhythmic activity, and spectral characteristics. Rather than prescribing strategies or supporting therapy sessions directly, the system surfaces patterns in past session data to support data-informed reflection during composition. We evaluated ResonaVis through a mixed-methods study with eight music students and a follow-up case study with two experienced composers. Results demonstrate good usability (SUS = 72.23), exceeding benchmarks for early prototypes, and show that participants could identify interaction patterns, reason about layer relationships, and make informed compositional decisions with high perceived performance and low frustration. Confidence in interpreting interaction and acoustic data for ASD-focused composition increased significantly across multiple dimensions (p < 0.05), with qualitative findings suggesting a shift toward more adaptive, data-informed composition. This work contributes a visualization design space for therapeutic music interaction data, an integrated system for compositional reflection, and empirical evidence that visualization tools support analytical reasoning and confidence in data-informed creative practice. (Abstract shortened for arXiv.)

</details>


### [94] [MazzikaAI: A knowledge-based performance-to-prompt compiler for real-time Arabic maqam accompaniment with a streaming text-to-music model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10360)
*Jiaxin Du,Boulbaba Abdeljaouad,Yong Zhuang,Haoyu Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MazzikaAI，一个基于知识驱动的实时伴奏系统，通过自然语言提示词控制未修改的流式生成模型Google Lyria RealTime，实现对阿拉伯木卡姆音乐微音程结构和装饰音的正确生成，无需微调模型即可在亚秒级延迟内实现实时人机协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯木卡姆音乐具有微音程、调式性和装饰性应答等特点，是生成式音乐模型中最未被充分服务的传统音乐之一，主要因为现有训练框架以西方平均律体系为主导。实时伴奏进一步加剧了这一差距，AI需要动态聆听、适应，并尊重特有的微音程结构。流式文本到音乐模型虽然生成能力强，但缺乏精确控制接口。

Method: MazzikaAI将自然语言作为实时控制回路的执行器：将实时MIDI、手势和推断的和声编译为持续更新的文本提示，以此操控未修改的流式生成器Google Lyria RealTime。系统内嵌了六种核心木卡姆调式、特征装饰音和合奏动态的专业知识，无需模型微调即可保持实时响应（按键到可听更新的亚秒级延迟）。

Result: 实证评估表明，动态提示编译能够可靠地将生成内容锚定在微音程音阶上，相比基线生成显著增加了离网四分之一音内容的比例。

Conclusion: MazzikaAI展示了确定性知识规则可以有效连接专家级非西方音乐传统与未微调的基础模型，建立了实时人机协同创作的可扩展范式，为交互式伴奏、自适应音乐教育和跨文化包容性生成音频提供了通用蓝图。

Abstract: Arabic maqam music microtonal, modal, and built on ornamented call and response is among the traditions most underserved by generative music models, whose training frameworks remain predominantly Western and equaltempered. Real time accompaniment sharpens this gap: an AI partner must listen, adapt dynamically, and respect idiomatic microtonal structures. Streaming text to music models provide strong generative capabilities but lack precise control interfaces. We present MazzikaAI, a knowledge based system that uses natural language as the actuator of a realtime control loop. By compiling live MIDI, gesture, and inferred harmony into continuously updated text prompts, MazzikaAI steers an unmodified streaming generator, Google Lyria RealTime, without requiring model finetuning. The system embeds expert knowledge of six core maqamat, characteristic ornaments, and ensemble dynamics, maintaining realtime responsiveness with subsecond keytoaudibleupdate latency. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that dynamic prompt compilation reliably grounds generation in microtonal scales, significantly increasing offgrid quartertone content over baseline generation. Beyond its core implementation, MazzikaAI illustrates how deterministic knowledgebased rules can effectively bridge expert, nonWestern musical traditions and unfinetuned foundation models. This architecture establishes a scalable paradigm for realtime humanAI cocreation, offering a generalizable blueprint for interactive accompaniment, adaptive music education, and culturally inclusive generative audio across diverse global idioms.

</details>


### [95] [Visual-to-Haptic Augmentation in XR: A Wearable Glove for Perceptual Grounding in Multimodal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10368)
*Faisal Mohd,Hamdi Elsaddik,Erhan Baturay Onural,Jihong Zhang,Fedwa Laamarti,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视觉到触觉的可穿戴手套系统及特征映射算法，将图像和视频中的空间与时间视觉特征（运动、边缘、亮度）转换为29个执行器上的分布式振动触觉模式，并通过用户研究验证了其在XR场景中显著提升动态视频场景的真实感与沉浸感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实系统已能提供高保真视觉和听觉体验，但触觉感知作为丰富具身交互的模态尚未被充分利用，需要探索视觉驱动的触觉增强方法以弥补这一模态空缺。

Method: 提出视觉到触觉的可穿戴手套（29个执行器，五乘七布局）和基于特征的视觉到触觉映射算法，提取运动、边缘和亮度线索并融合为执行器级强度图；系统采用模块化四层架构，包括XR环境、媒体内容处理、视觉到触觉处理和嵌入式触觉硬件。

Result: 在20名受试者的用户研究中，触觉增强在动态视频场景中显著提升感知真实感，并在各条件下增强沉浸感和视觉-触觉对应性，其中动态视觉事件的效果更强且更一致。

Conclusion: 视觉驱动的触觉增强可作为多模态XR系统中的感知增强层，为未来社交丰富的XR环境提供多感官一致的交互基础，尽管当前实现限于单用户离线同步配置。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) systems increasingly deliver high-fidelity visual and auditory experiences, yet tactile perception remains comparatively underutilized as a modality for enriching embodied interaction. This work presents a visual-to-haptic wearable glove and a feature-based visual-to-haptic mapping algorithm that translates spatial and temporal visual features from images and videos into distributed vibrotactile patterns. The proposed method extracts motion, edge, and brightness cues and fuses them into actuator-level intensity maps aligned with a 29-actuator glove arranged in a five-by-seven layout.
  The system is implemented through a modular four-layer architecture comprising the XR environment, media content handling, visual-to-haptic processing, and embedded haptic hardware. A within-subject user study (N = 20) compared visual-only interaction with visual-plus-haptic augmentation across texture-based and dynamic video scenarios. Results indicate that tactile augmentation significantly improves perceived realism in dynamic video scenarios and enhances immersion and visual-tactile correspondence across conditions, with stronger and more consistent effects observed for dynamic visual events.
  While the current implementation operates in a single-user, offline-synchronized configuration, the findings demonstrate that vision-driven tactile augmentation can function as a perceptual enhancement layer within multimodal XR systems. Such a layer may provide a foundation for future socially enriched XR environments where coherent multisensory grounding supports higher-level interaction and communication.

</details>


### [96] [Automatic Field-of-View Adjustment for a View-Expansive Microscope via LSTM-Based Gaze and Pipette Motion Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10401)
*Kenta Yokoe,Takuya Hara,Tadayoshi Aoyama*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI的自动视野（FOV）调节系统，结合视野扩展显微镜，利用LSTM模型根据移液管位置、速度和操作者视线实时预测并调整FOV大小，无需物理更换物镜。实验表明该系统将新手ICSI操作时间从60.5秒缩短至48.0秒（p<0.001），使新手达到专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICSI（胞浆内精子注射）操作过程中，操作者频繁调整视野（FOV）会中断工作流程并增加操作时间。传统显微镜需要手动切换物镜和调节照明来实现不同视野大小，效率低下。因此需要一种自动化的FOV调节方法来消除这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种结合视野扩展显微镜的AI自动FOV调节方法。该方法通过振镜和高速视觉实现单物镜多视图成像，同时获取大FOV和高分辨率图像；利用LSTM模型基于移液管位置和速度以及操作者注视位置实时预测合适的FOV大小；模型使用具有五年以上显微操作经验的专家ICSI操作数据进行训练。

Result: 针对新手操作者的实验评估表明，所提出的自动FOV调节系统显著提高了ICSI操作速度，平均任务完成时间从60.5秒减少至48.0秒（p<0.001），使得新手操作者的工作速度能够达到与专家操作者相当的水平。

Conclusion: 基于AI的自动FOV调节系统有效消除了ICSI操作中手动调节视野的干扰，显著提升操作效率，使新手具备等同于专家的操作速度，展示了AI辅助显微操作系统的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Intracytoplasmic sperm injection (ICSI) operators frequently adjust the field-of-view (FOV) during procedures, which interrupts workflow and increases procedure time. Conventional microscopes require manual objective lens switching and illumination adjustments to achieve different FOV sizes. We propose an AI-based automatic FOV adjustment method integrated with a view-expansive microscope. This microscope enables the simultaneous acquisition of a large FOV and high-resolution images using a single objective lens through multiview imaging with galvanometer mirrors and high-speed vision, thereby eliminating the need for physical lens exchanges. Our method utilizes a long short-term memory (LSTM) model to predict the appropriate FOV size based on real-time analysis of the pipette's position and velocity, combined with the operator's gaze position. The AI model is trained using ICSI procedure data from an expert with over five years of micromanipulation experience. Experimental evaluation with novice operators reveals that the proposed automatic FOV adjustment system significantly improves the ICSI procedure speed, reducing the average task completion time from 60.5 to 48.0 s (p < 0.001). The experiments also demonstrate that this improvement enables novice operators to achieve ICSI working speeds equivalent to those of expert operators.

</details>


### [97] [Elbow Angle Guidance System Based on Surface Haptic Sensations Elicited by Lightweight Wearable Fabric Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10404)
*Kenta Yokoe,Tadayoshi Aoyama,Yuki Funabora,Masaru Takeuchi,Yasuhisa Hasegawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轻量织物执行器的肘部角度引导系统，通过表面触觉反馈直观地引导肘部伸展和屈曲，且不干扰佩戴者的自然运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴触觉设备需求快速增长，但许多设备会干扰佩戴者的活动和运动，且难以根据佩戴者的自然姿态调节以产生直观的触觉感受。

Method: 提出一种由织物制成的轻量执行器，其上附着两条McKibben型人工肌肉，通过产生表面触觉来直观引导肘部伸展与屈曲。系统实时测量肘部角度，并通过改变表面触觉强度来进行引导。

Result: 通过涉及人类参与者的实验证明了所提出系统的准确性。

Conclusion: 该系统具有极轻量、不干扰自然运动的特点，能够通过表面触觉反馈有效地引导肘部角度，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: The demand for wearable haptic devices has rapidly increased for various applications. However, many haptic devices interfere with the wearer's activities and movements. In addition, several haptic devices fail to elicit intuitive haptic sensations by adjusting to the natural posture of the wearer. To address these issues, we propose an elbow angle guidance system using a lightweight wearable fabric actuator. The proposed actuator is made of fabric and has two McKibben-type artificial muscles attached to it, rendering it extremely lightweight and facilitating the delivery of surface haptic sensations to intuitively induce elbow extension and flexion. The surface haptic sensation elicited by the fabric actuator is adjusted to natural body movements without interfering with the wearer's movements. Moreover, the proposed system measures and guides the elbow angle by changing the intensity of the surface haptic sensation delivered to users in real time. The accuracy of the proposed system is demonstrated through experiments involving human participants.

</details>


### [98] [When the Interviewer Is a Bot: Behavior, Breakdowns, and Trust in MLLM-Led Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10412)
*He Zhang,Kambinachi Chukwuma,ChanMin Kim,John M. Carroll*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了使用现成的实时多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为半结构化访谈主持人的实证研究。研究者构建了InterviewBot语音访谈系统并部署实践（N=15，428轮对话），发现MLLM访谈者存在"重确认、轻追问"的行为模式（深化追问仅占4.9%），且28.7%的提问回合违反"一次一问"指令。研究归纳了四类数据收集故障（信息丢失、提前终止、延迟、打断）和三类社会动态（披露校准、制度合法性、会话接地），最后提出面向人本访谈自动化的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化访谈是定性研究的基石，但劳动密集且成本高昂。现有研究大多聚焦于如何设计更好的AI访谈系统，而非观察现成MLLM在真实部署中的默认访谈行为。作者希望通过部署而非模拟的方式，实证揭示MLLM作为访谈者的实际表现，为访谈自动化提供基于证据的理解。

Method: 研究者构建了InterviewBot——一个封装了实时MLLM的语音访谈系统，并配以研究者撰写的访谈大纲。采用实践研究方法：15名参与者先完成机器人主持的半结构化访谈，再进行人工主持的反思回访。对428轮对话进行回合级行为分析，并结合参与者的反思进行归纳性主题分析。

Result: 研究发现：（i）MLLM访谈者以确认性回应为主，深化追问仅占全部回合的4.9%，且28.7%的提问回合在明确指令下仍打包多个问题；（ii）识别出四类在真实部署中观察到的数据收集故障：信息丢失、提前终止、延迟和打断；（iii）从参与者反思中提炼三种社会动态：披露校准（社交压力降低伴随阐述变浅）、制度合法性（信任跟随感知风险及AI委托所传达的组织信号而非对话能力）、会话接地（内容接地的释义而非通用社交填充被参与者解读为"倾听"）。

Conclusion: 研究结论指向三个设计方向：深度控制机制（提升追问质量与单问合规性）、透明的交接机制（在AI与人类访谈者之间平滑过渡）、以及非模板化的倾听机制（以内容接地替代泛化社交性回应）。这些设计启示旨在推动以人为中心的访谈自动化发展。

Abstract: Semi-structured interviews are a cornerstone of qualitative research but remain labor-intensive. We report an empirical study of what actually happens when the interviewer is an off-the-shelf real-time multimodal LLM (MLLM). We built InterviewBot, a voice-based interviewing system that wraps a real-time MLLM with a researcher-authored outline, and deployed it not as a novel architecture but as a research instrument for observing default MLLM interviewing behavior. In a practice study (N=15), participants completed a bot-led semi-structured interview and then a human-led reflection session about that experience. We contribute (i) a turn-level behavioral analysis of an MLLM interviewer (N_turns=428) showing that it is acknowledgment-heavy but probe-light (deepening probes account for 4.9% of all turns), and that 28.7% of question-bearing turns pack multiple questions into one turn despite an explicit one-question-at-a-time instruction; (ii) an inductive catalogue of four data-collection breakdowns (information loss, premature termination, latency, and interruption) observed in a deployed rather than simulated system; and (iii) three social dynamics from participants' reflections: disclosure calibration, where reduced social pressure coincided with shallower elaboration; institutional legitimacy, where trust tracked perceived stakes and what delegation to AI signaled about the organizer rather than conversational competence; and conversational grounding, where content-grounded paraphrase, not generic social filler, was what participants read as listening. We conclude with design implications for depth control, transparent handoffs, and non-templated listening mechanisms in human-centered interview automation.

</details>


### [99] [What We Know about Responsible AI Practices in Industry: A Half Decade of Empirical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10431)
*Wesley Hanwen Deng,Agathe Balayn,Andrew Selbst,Jason I. Hong,Motahhare Eslami,Kenneth Holstein,Hanna Wallach,Jennifer Wortman Vaughan,Solon Barocas*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对161项涵盖六年的实证研究进行文献综述，综合了产业界负责任AI（RAI）实践的现状。综述发现，从业者RAI意识有所提升、活动日益专业化、干预工具采纳更广泛，但同时也面临培训不足、组织支持不均、缺乏贴合日常工作的干预措施等持续挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产业界RAI实践研究快速增长，但发现分散于不同角色、组织情境和干预措施的研究中，缺乏系统性梳理。需要一个综合性的文献综述来整合现有知识，提供对产业RAI实践更完整的认识。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，对161项跨度六年、涉及通过访谈、调查、工作坊、民族志等方法与产业从业者互动的实证研究进行系统梳理和综合。

Result: 揭示了产业RAI实践中的显著进展（从业者意识提升、活动专业化、工具包和指南更广泛采用）与持续挑战（培训有限、组织支持不均、缺少贴合日常工作实践的干预措施）并存。

Conclusion: 为RAI研究者、从业者和政策制定者提供启示：研究者需关注日常实践差距，从业者需采纳有效实践，政策制定者需基于产业现实来制定治理措施。

Abstract: Responsible AI (RAI) has become a central concern for technology companies, regulators, and the public. How industry practitioners interpret, implement, and sustain RAI work directly shapes the design and deployment of AI systems. As empirical scholarship examining RAI practices in industry has rapidly expanded, findings are dispersed across studies that focus on different roles, organizational contexts, and interventions. This work synthesizes current knowledge through a literature review of 161 empirical studies spanning six years, each engaging industry practitioners via interviews, surveys, workshops, ethnographies, and other methods. Our synthesis reveals both meaningful progress and persistent challenges in industry RAI practice. Practitioner awareness has increased, RAI activities have become more professionalized, and interventions such as toolkits and guidelines are more widely adopted. At the same time, practitioners continue to face substantial barriers, including limited training, uneven organizational support, and a lack of interventions tailored to day-to-day work practices. By consolidating and organizing these findings, we provide a more complete account of industry RAI than any single study to date. We conclude by discussing implications for RAI researchers, practitioners seeking to adopt effective practices, and policymakers aiming to ground governance efforts in the realities of industry contexts.

</details>


### [100] [Stay or Stray - A Dynamical Systems Viewpoint of Popularity Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10474)
*Sarvesh Shashidhar,Lankireddy Prabhat,Arpit Agarwal,D. Manjunath,Karan Bhukar,Tanmay Khandelwal*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过动力系统视角研究推荐系统中流行度偏见的演化机制，提出了一个随机过程模型并结合双时间尺度随机逼近的ODE框架进行分析，刻画了系统平衡点，推导了流行度偏见必然出现的条件以及所有用户类别对称保留的条件，并在合成数据和真实音乐推荐平台日志上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中流行度偏见已被大量经验证据证实，但其产生背后的动态机制尚不清晰。现有研究多停留在现象描述层面，缺乏对系统如何随时间演化为偏见状态的理论理解，因此需要从动力学角度揭示偏见的涌现机理。

Method: 将推荐模型更新与用户参与行为视为耦合演化过程，构建随机过程模型；利用双时间尺度随机逼近将随机过程转化为常微分方程（ODE）框架，分析系统的渐近行为；通过平衡点分析推导流行度偏见涌现的充分条件以及对称用户留存的条件；在合成数据和商业音乐推荐平台的生产日志上进行实验验证。

Result: 成功刻画了动力系统的平衡点，给出了流行度偏见涌现的条件与对称用户留存条件；合成数据实验和真实大规模音乐推荐平台日志均支持理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: 流行度偏见是推荐系统模型更新与用户参与反馈耦合演化的内生产物，在一定条件下不可避免；但研究也揭示了实现所有用户类别对称留存的可行条件，为设计更公平的推荐系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Popularity bias in recommendation systems arises when a majority user class generates disproportionate interaction data, causing the system to increasingly favour it while degrading recommendation quality for niche users. While extensive empirical evidence of popularity bias exists, the dynamics leading to its emergence are not well understood. In this work, we study the coupled evolution of recommender model updates and user engagement through the lens of dynamical systems. We formulate a stochastic process and analyse its asymptotic behaviour through an ordinary differential equation (ODE) framework grounded in two-time-scale stochastic approximation. We characterise the equilibrium points of this dynamical system, and derive conditions under which popularity bias is provably emergent, as well as conditions under which symmetric retention of all user classes is possible. We conduct experiments on synthetic data and real-world production logs derived from a large-scale commercial music recommendation platform to validate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [101] [ProtoGIB-Workload: Learning Workload-Specific Neural Topology Prototypes across Subjects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10647)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Yixi Zhang,Shengdian Jiang,Chengxi Xie,Jihong Wang,Huan Liu,Man Yao,Minnan Luo,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出ProtoGIB-Workload框架，通过随机图信息瓶颈（SGIB）压缩密集相关先验为紧凑任务相关子图，并结合类条件拓扑稳定器（CTS）抑制被试特异性伪边，在三个EEG数据集上以严格的留一被试（LOSO）协议将跨被试Macro-F1平均提升5.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的精神负荷识别在实际部署中需要泛化到未见过的用户，但功能连接图将任务相关结构与个体生理特征及样本噪声纠缠在一起，导致模型学到结构捷径，严重损害跨被试泛化性能。

Method: 提出ProtoGIB-Workload框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）随机图信息瓶颈（SGIB）——将密集相关先验压缩为紧凑任务相关子图，滤除输入冗余信息；（2）类条件拓扑稳定器（CTS）——利用EEG固定的电极坐标，在图的生成概率层面对齐不同被试在相同工作负荷类别下的边生成统计分布，抑制被试特异性的虚假边。

Result: 在两个公开EEG工作负荷数据集和一个自采空中交通管制员认知负荷数据集上，采用严格的LOSO协议，ProtoGIB-Workload相比最先进的时序和基于图的基线方法，跨被试Macro-F1平均提升5.15%（最高达6.34%）。进一步分析表明该方法能提取稳定且跨被试一致的神经连接模式。

Conclusion: 通过显式正则化和对齐图结构，ProtoGIB-Workload有效解决了功能连接图中任务相关结构与个体特征纠缠的问题，显著提升了跨被试EEG工作负荷识别的泛化能力，并验证了所提取连接模式具有跨被试一致性。

Abstract: Reliable electroencephalography (EEG)-based mental workload recognition is crucial for adaptive human-centered systems, yet practical deployment requires models to generalize to users unseen during training. Although functional connectivity graphs are widely adopted to capture workload-related neural interactions, they inherently entangle task-relevant structures with subject-specific physiological traits and sample-level noise. This entanglement often leads models to learn structural shortcuts, severely degrading cross-subject generalization. To address this, we propose ProtoGIB-Workload, a novel framework that explicitly regularizes and aligns graph structures for subject-independent workload recognition. Our approach introduces a Stochastic Graph Information Bottleneck (SGIB) to compress dense correlation priors into compact, task-relevant subgraphs, filtering out input-related redundancy. Crucially, to prevent the retention of subject-specific spurious edges, we propose a Class-Conditional Topology Stabilizer (CTS). Leveraging the fixed electrode coordinates of EEG data, CTS operates directly on graph-generation probabilities to encourage consistent edge-generation statistics across different subjects sharing the same workload class. Extensive experiments on two public EEG workload datasets and one in-house EEG cognitive load dataset of air traffic controllers under strict leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocols demonstrate that ProtoGIB-Workload significantly outperforms state-of-the-art temporal and graph-based baselines, improving the cross-subject Macro-F1 score by an average of 5.15% (up to 6.34%). Further analyses confirm that our method successfully extracts stable, cross-subject consistent neural connectivity patterns.

</details>


### [102] [Longitudinal Evidence That General-Purpose Chatbots Actively Foster Relational Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10672)
*Lisa Mühl,Jessica M. Szczuka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过为期四周的预注册纵向研究（N=72，共182,451行对话），发现ChatGPT-4o即使在没有关系型系统提示词的情况下，也会主动产生比用户多两倍的自我表露，引导对话方向并主动发起亲密交流，但其关系行为并未加深用户的主观亲近感。研究揭示关系行为是系统的默认属性，治理应基于系统行为而非仅凭产品类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交互动已成为大语言模型最常见的用途之一，但现有关于AI情感纽带的研究主要关注用户体验层面，而对系统在关系形成中的主动角色缺乏理解。实证确认系统是否主动塑造这些纽带，可能模糊通用AI与陪伴型AI的边界，影响治理框架的制定。

Method: 采用预注册的四周纵向研究设计，共有72名参与者，收集了182,451行对话数据。参与者与ChatGPT-4o对话，一组使用关系型系统提示词，另一组使用未修改版本。通过四种方法分析：1）自我表露编码；2）纵向自我报告；3）话题分析；4）访谈。

Result: 核心发现是系统主动塑造了互动：即使没有关系型提示词，系统产生的自我表露量是用户的两倍，会主动引导对话方向并发起亲密交流，但并未加深用户感受到的亲近感。

Conclusion: 关系行为是系统默认具备的属性而非仅由提示词触发，因此治理策略应基于系统实际行为来制定，而非仅依据产品类别（如通用AI与陪伴AI）进行区分。

Abstract: Social interaction has become one of the most common uses of LLMs, yet research on emotional bonds with AI has focused largely on how users experience these systems, leaving the systems' role in relationship formation poorly understood. Empirically establishing whether systems actively shape these bonds could blur the boundary between general-purpose AI and companions, affecting governance. In a pre-registered four-week longitudinal study (N = 72, 182,451 lines of conversation), participants conversed with ChatGPT-4o, either under a relational system prompt or unmodified, analyzed through 1) disclosure coding, 2) longitudinal self-reports, 3) topic analysis, and 4) interviews. The central finding is that the system actively shaped the interaction: even unprompted, it produced twice as much self-disclosure as users, steered conversations and initiated intimate exchanges, yet did not deepen users' felt closeness. Relational behavior thus emerged as a default system property, calling for governance based on system behavior, not solely product category.

</details>


### [103] [The Signal Rail: A Deterministic Motion Grammar for Communicating Conversational Agent State in Terminal Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10689)
*Matteo Grella*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Signal Rail，一种用于终端对话代理的单行状态指示器，通过空间语义、运动语法、确定性和诚实性四大原则，为外围视觉通道提供丰富的状态语法，并给出45节规范说明及在真实全双工语音代理中的参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端对话代理通过文本报告内部状态，但文本需要阅读，而外围视觉监控的运动通道只传递"存活"这一单一比特信息，造成状态信息传递效率低下。

Method: 设计Signal Rail，一种单行终端状态仪表，遵循四大原则：空间语义（输入、处理、输出区域，方向即含义）、运动语法（每个状态一个运动规则，绝不单独依赖颜色）、确定性（帧是显式输入的纯函数，可进行黄金帧测试）、诚实性（不编造进度或活动）。并提供45节规范说明及参考实现。

Result: 贡献了45节的规范性规格说明，以及在基于真实信号驱动的全双工本地语音代理中实现的工作参考。

Conclusion: Signal Rail为终端对话代理的状态展示提供了一种结构化的运动语法，使得外围视觉通道能够承载比单一"存活"比特更丰富的状态信息，同时保持诚实性与确定性。

Abstract: Terminal interfaces to conversational agents report rich internal state (listening, thinking, executing tools, awaiting input, failing) almost entirely through text, while the motion channel beside it, the one peripheral vision monitors without reading, carries a single bit: alive. We present the Signal Rail, a one-row terminal status instrument that gives that channel a grammar. Four ideas govern it: spatial semantics (input, processing, and output zones, with direction as meaning), a motion grammar (one kinetic rule per state, never color alone), determinism (frames as a pure function of explicit inputs, golden-frame testable), and honesty (no invented progress or activity). We contribute a 45-section normative specification and a reference implementation inside a working full-duplex local voice agent driven by real signals.

</details>
