TL;DR: 本文从马尔可夫生成元的角度研究了Lévy驱动的生成模型的反向采样问题。与传统的扩散模型不同，Lévy驱动动力学涉及无限跳跃活动，导致其反向过程是非局部的，难以仅用分数信息刻画。作者推导出反向跳跃成分在一般情况下是一个由非局部密度比控制的状态依赖马尔可夫跳跃过程，并据此提出了一个将动力学分解为扩散、小跳跃和大跳跃三部分的逆向采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型的反向过程依赖分数匹配，但Lévy驱动的动力学包含无限跳跃活动，其反向过程是非局部的，无法仅用分数信息简单刻画。因此需要从更基础的马尔可夫生成元角度重新分析逆向采样问题。

Method: 通过分析前向和反向生成元，推导出反向跳跃成分的结构特征，进而设计一个将动力学分解为扩散、小跳跃和大跳跃成分的结构化逆向采样器。对一类具有对称α稳定跳跃分量的各向同性线性Lévy SDE，采用神经网络仅用于摊销大跳跃活动的速率，跳跃幅度由解析推导的条件分布生成。同时引入高效实现技术以避免高维积分和采样。

Result: 将该采样器适配到近似观测引导采样，并应用于混合高斯和脉冲噪声下的OFDM-SISO信道估计。仿真结果表明该方法的估计性能稳健，在复杂度和性能之间取得了良好的折衷。

Conclusion: 本文提出了基于马尔可夫生成元的Lévy驱动生成模型反向采样理论框架，通过分解跳跃成分并利用解析条件分布生成跳跃幅度，提高了采样器的可解释性和可控性，并在实际通信应用场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: This paper studies inverse sampling for Lévy-driven generative models from the perspective of Markov generators. Unlike conventional diffusion models, Lévy-driven dynamics involve infinite jump activities, which makes their reverse process nonlocal and difficult to characterize using score information alone. We address this challenge by analyzing the forward and reversed generators. It is derived that the reversed jump component generally becomes a state-dependent Markov jump process governed by a nonlocal density ratio. This observation motivates a structured reverse sampler that decomposes the dynamics into diffusion, small jump, and large jump components. Based on this characterization, we develop a computationally tractable sampler for a class of isotropic linear Lévy SDEs with symmetric $α$-stable jump components. For the jump component, the neural network is used only to amortize the rate of large jump activities, while jump amplitudes are generated from analytically derived conditional distributions, which improves interpretability and controllability. Efficient implementation techniques are further introduced under this setting to avoid expensive high-dimensional integration and sampling. The sampler is further adapted to approximate observation-guided sampling and applied to OFDM-SISO channel estimation under mixed Gaussian and impulsive noise. Simulations show robust estimation performance with a favorable tradeoff between complexity and performance.

</details>


### [139] [Dreamer-SAC: Off-Policy Learning in Latent World Models for Sample-Efficient Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10386)
*Jiazhuo Li,Linjiang Cao,Qi Liu,Xi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dreamer-SAC框架，将循环状态空间世界模型与离线软演员-评论家算法相结合，在潜空间中训练策略，通过短时域生成轨迹和多目标监督实现自动驾驶的高效强化学习，在减少真实环境交互次数的同时优于DreamerV3、SAC和PPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的样本高效强化学习受限于数据效率与模型偏差之间的权衡。世界模型虽然减少了对昂贵环境交互的依赖，但基于学习动力学的策略优化对预测误差十分敏感，亟需一种方法在利用世界模型的同时缓解模型偏差问题。

Method: 提出Dreamer-SAC框架，核心组件包括：(1)循环状态空间世界模型用于学习潜空间动态；(2)在潜空间中直接训练的离线软演员-评论家(SAC)算法；(3)真实交互与短时域生成轨迹的组合使用；(4)n步目标估计与多目标监督机制。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景中（涵盖驾驶效率与安全目标），框架一致性地优于DreamerV3、SAC和PPO等基线方法，且以显著更少的真实环境交互实现更优性能。实验发现滚动时域与策略性能呈倒U型关系，短时域潜空间滚动在额外训练信号与累积模型偏差之间取得最佳权衡；n步目标估计优于单步时序差分目标。

Conclusion: 短时域潜空间滚动结合n步目标估计是缓解世界模型偏差、提升样本效率的有效策略，Dreamer-SAC为自动驾驶强化学习提供了一种兼顾数据效率与策略性能的实用框架。

Abstract: Sample-efficient reinforcement learning for autonomous driving is often limited by the trade-off between data efficiency and model bias. While world models reduce the reliance on costly environment interactions, policy optimization over learned dynamics remains sensitive to prediction errors. This paper proposes the Dreamer-SAC framework, which integrates a recurrent state-space world model with an off-policy soft actor-critic algorithm trained directly in latent space. The framework uses a combination of real interactions and short-horizon generated trajectories with n-step target estimation and multi-objective supervision. Evaluated in autonomous driving scenarios with objectives encompassing driving efficiency and safety, the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative reinforcement learning baselines, including DreamerV3, SAC, and PPO, while achieving improved performance with substantially fewer real environment interactions. Experiments reveal an inverted-U relationship between rollout horizon and policy performance, where short-horizon latent rollouts achieve the best trade-off between additional training signals and accumulated model bias. Furthermore, n-step target estimation demonstrates more effectiveness over one-step temporal-difference targets in exploiting predicted experience for value learning.

</details>


### [140] [Share First, Route What Remains: A Unified Framework for Token-Adaptive MoE Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10392)
*Gongli Zhang,Zhulin Liu,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniF-MoE统一框架，遵循"先共享、再路由"原则，将MoE专家划分为对齐块，动态决定共享块数量、共享内容及剩余专家数量，在DomainBed和GLUE上优于现有静态和动态MoE，同时降低计算量、延迟和内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE设计（共享专家、细粒度方法、动态路由）通常独立进行，忽视了共享提取与剩余专家容量之间的依赖关系。论文旨在揭示这一依赖并据此设计统一框架。

Method: 将稀疏升级的前馈专家分解为键值通道，通过共享需求分数决定共享块数量与路径权重，键原型选择共享内容，累积路由质量决定剩余专家数量；引入Gram正则化器分离并规范化路由嵌入，促进多样化路由方向和稀疏专家重叠。

Result: 在DomainBed和GLUE基准上，UniF-MoE相比代表性的静态和动态MoE取得更好的预测性能，同时降低激活计算量、推理延迟和内存占用。

Conclusion: 共享优先、路由剩余的统一设计原则有效提升了MoE模型的性能与效率，验证了共享与专家容量之间存在可建模的依赖关系。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models have recently moved beyond routing a fixed number of complete experts. Shared-expert designs preserve reusable knowledge, fine-grained methods vary computation within experts, and dynamic routers adapt the number of active experts. Yet these decisions are usually made independently, overlooking a basic dependency: extracting reusable computation changes both what remains and how much expert capacity the remainder needs. We study this dependency by decomposing sparsely upcycled feed-forward experts into key-value channels. Co-activated experts align at a subset of value positions; removing these positions changes expert preference; and greater shared coverage is associated with lower residual expert demand. These observations lead to one principle: share first, then route what remains. We instantiate it in UniF-MoE, a unified framework for token-adaptive MoE computation. Each expert is partitioned into aligned blocks. A shared-demand score sets the shared block count and pathway weight, key prototypes select the shared content, and the complementary demand determines the residual expert count through cumulative routing mass. A Gram regularizer separates and normalizes router embeddings, promoting diverse routing directions, sparse expert overlap, and a simple routing geometry. Experiments on DomainBed and GLUE show that this unified design improves predictive performance over representative static and dynamic MoEs while reducing activated computation, inference latency, and memory. Code is available at https://github.com/existence0420/UniF-MoE.

</details>


### [141] [ELVAE: Evidential Learning-Based Variational Autoencoder for Uncertainty-Aware Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10398)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ELVAE，一种基于证据学习的变分自编码器，为每个潜在坐标赋予输入相关的正态-逆伽马后验分布，从而显式区分潜在位置不确定性与潜在周围的变异性。这种不确定性可直接用于生成过程：低不确定性锚点生成更可靠的样本，高不确定性锚点可用于压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE只能从概率潜在表征中采样，但无法区分关于潜在位置的不确定性与潜在周围的变异性。这种区分对于不确定性感知的生成至关重要，但目前缺乏一种能够显式建模并在生成过程中利用潜在位置不确定性的方法。

Method: ELVAE通过正态-逆伽马后验分布为每个潜在坐标建模，构建输入相关的层次结构。目标函数是精确的ELBO，并证明需要对完整层次结构进行直接正则化，因为仅边际化潜在律无法识别不确定性分解。

Result: 在MNIST生成实验中，冻结外部分类器显示不确定性能清晰分层生成数字的语义可靠性。零位移对照实验表明大部分效果源于锚点可重新生成的可靠性，另有较小但独立的部分来自不确定性缩放的扰动本身。效果仅在类内不确定性排序下成立，且幅度随随机种子变化。

Conclusion: 学习到的潜在位置不确定性可作为实用的控制变量用于不确定性感知生成，将锚点可靠性与扰动诱导失败分离开来，为生成过程中的不确定性调控提供了新工具。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders generate samples from probabilistic latent representations but do not distinguish uncertainty about the latent location from variability around it. We formulate ELVAE, an evidential learning-based VAE in which each latent coordinate is governed by an input-dependent normal-inverse-gamma posterior. This hierarchy yields an explicit latent-location uncertainty that can be used during generation, not merely reported after inference: low-uncertainty anchors support more reliable synthetic samples, while high-uncertainty anchors can be deliberately exploited for stress testing. The objective is an exact evidence lower bound, and we show that direct regularization of the full hierarchy is required, since the marginalized latent law alone cannot identify the uncertainty decomposition. In an MNIST generation pilot with a frozen external classifier, this uncertainty clearly stratified the semantic reliability of generated digits. A zero-displacement control revealed that most of the effect reflects how reliably an anchor can be re-generated, while a smaller but distinct component is attributable to uncertainty-scaled perturbation itself. The effect holds only under within-class uncertainty ranking, and its magnitude varies across seeds. These findings support the learned latent-location uncertainty as a practical control variable for uncertainty-aware generation, separating anchor reliability from perturbation-induced failure.

</details>


### [142] [Do Judges Behave Like Algorithms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10400)
*Riya Manchanda,Eric Chen,Chloe Zhu,Cynthia Rudin,Brandon Garrett,Songman Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过研究德克萨斯州哈里斯县轻罪保释听证会中治安法官的决策行为，发现法官的行为在很大程度上类似于可预测的算法——其决策可由小而可解释的公式捕获。然而，在部分案件中法官之间存在显著差异，导致类似被告受到不一致和不平等对待。识别算法无法解释的司法决策案例，有助于改进司法系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能和算法正在被部署到司法系统中，以往的研究多聚焦于法官是否应使用算法，但本文提出了一个反向问题：法官是否已经在按照类似算法的规则行事？如果法官的决策基于离散和静态因素（如犯罪史、年龄和指控类型）且高度一致，则可以通过规则改善司法行为；如果依赖无法从法院数据中识别的个性化信息，则基于标准（而非规则）的决策将更难理解和改进。

Method: 使用哈里斯县轻罪保释听证会的法院数据，为每位法官训练机器学习模型，通过变量重要性指标衡量各法官决策中的关键变量，并分析相似案件在不同法官间的结果一致性。

Result: 研究发现法官通常表现出算法式行为：其决策可由小而可解释的公式捕获。但部分案件中法官之间存在实质性差异，导致相似被告受到不一致和不平等的对待。

Conclusion: 识别算法无法解释司法决策的案例，可以将关注点集中在需要个性化标准而非规则解释结果的决定上，从而改进司法系统，减少不公平对待。

Abstract: What if judges already behave like algorithms? As artificial intelligence and algorithms are deployed in many settings, including the judicial system, many have debated whether judges should be allowed to rely on them. Instead, we ask whether judges follow predictable, algorithmic-like rules already. If judges already follow consistent, formula-like rules based on discrete and static factors such as criminal history, age, and charge type, then judicial behavior may be improved. However, if judges rely on individualized information that cannot be identified through court data, then standards-based decision-making may be more challenging to understand or improve. This work explores these questions by studying judicial decision-making in misdemeanor bail hearings in Harris County, Texas. Using available court data, we investigate whether magistrate judges follow what resembles an algorithm; whether they consider the same variables in their decision-making; and whether they are consistent with themselves and with each other. To do this, we train machine learning models for each judge, measure variable importance metrics to determine important variables for each judge's decision-making, and analyze outcomes of similar cases for judges. Our results reveal that these judges generally behave algorithmically: their decisions can be captured by small, interpretable formulas. However, in some cases, judges differ substantially, leading to surprising inconsistency and unequal treatment across similar defendants. Identifying cases where algorithms do not explain judicial decision-making can improve the justice system by focusing attention on decisions where individualized standards, rather than rules, better explains outcomes.

</details>


### [143] [TideRL: Boosting Agentic RL Goodput with Readiness-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10402)
*Yanyu Ren,Xizheng Wang,Xiao Liu,Bowen Lv,Hanchen Zhang,Shudan Zhang,Hanyu Lai,Shuai Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TideRL是一个面向多轮智能体工作负载的就绪感知弹性强化学习系统，通过连续任务批处理、资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线和弹性资源扩展，将RL训练有效吞吐提升最高5.6倍（对比同步基线）和33%以上（对比异步基线），同时将KV缓存命中率提升1.58倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习正转向多轮智能体工作负载，其特点是rollout任务会反复暂停等待外部环境、以不断增长的上下文恢复运行、并以高度可变的时间结束。在这种场景下，GPU等待和重复的prefill重计算都是纯开销，训练有效吞吐比原始GPU占用率更重要，现有系统未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了三个核心组件：(1) 连续任务批处理(CTB)保留有价值的rollout状态；(2) 资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线(RA²P)根据就绪积压和到达间隔在解耦流式与聚合聚合之间进行选择；(3) 弹性资源扩展(ERS)利用相同的就绪信号在rollout和训练之间移动rank。

Result: 在纯文本和多模态智能体工作负载上，TideRL相比同步基线将RL训练有效吞吐提升最高5.6倍，相比异步基线提升超过33%，同时达到相似的任务性能。KV缓存命中率提升1.58倍，单步训练时间降低最高44.3%，总等待时间削减最高77.6%。

Conclusion: 就绪感知的弹性RL系统设计可以显著提升多轮智能体工作负载下的训练有效吞吐，通过减少GPU等待和prefill重计算开销，在保持任务性能的同时大幅提高系统效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models is moving toward multi-turn agentic workloads, where rollout tasks repeatedly pause for external environments, resume with growing contexts, and finish at highly variable times. In this setting, RL training goodput, measured by training throughput, matters more than raw GPU occupancy: GPU waiting and repeated prefill recomputation are pure overhead. We present TideRL, a readiness-aware elastic RL system with Continuous Task Batching, Resource-Aware Ref-Actor Pipelining, and Elastic Resource Scaling. CTB preserves useful rollout state, $\textrm{RA}^2\textrm{P}$ selects between decoupled streaming and colocated aggregation from the ready backlog and arrival interval, and ERS moves ranks between rollout and training using the same readiness signals. Across text-only and multi-modal agentic workloads, TideRL improves RL training goodput by up to 5.6$\times$ over synchronous baselines and over 33% over asynchronous baselines, while reaching similar task performance. It also improves KV cache hit rate by 1.58$\times$, reduces per-step training time by up to 44.3%, and cuts total waiting time by up to 77.6%.

</details>


### [144] [Actionable Hallucination Detection: Translating Latent Uncertainty into Agentic Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10430)
*Sanidhya Vijayvargiya,Rahul Lokesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Critic，一种轻量级LoRA适配器，通过重构Transformer残差流来放大潜在接地信号，在单序列内实现细粒度的自然语言幻觉检测，无需二次推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在执行任务时经常出现用户规格接地失败，即执行幻觉的、非期望的动作而非表达不确定性。已有检测方法要么无法定位幻觉，要么推理延迟过高，缺乏可操作的实时纠正手段。

Method: Latent Critic是一个轻量级低秩适配器（LoRA），与冻结的基础LLM生成过程并发运行，主动重构Transformer残差流——放大潜在接地信号并将其转化为同一序列内的局部自然语言反馈。通过完善基础模型的原生不确定性信号，在无二次推理循环开销的情况下实现可靠的细粒度检测。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama模型上，该方法在幻觉隔离上达到0.966 AUROC，定位准确率超过80%。显著优于同等规模的微调外部检测器、语义熵基线和被动内部探针。在ReAct闭环环境中作为低延迟护栏，能在执行前拦截幻觉并支持智能体自我纠正。

Conclusion: 通过操作潜在空间而非引入额外推理阶段，Latent Critic实现了高效、可定位的幻觉检测，在保持推理延迟可忽略的同时提供了可操作的局部反馈，有效防止智能体执行非期望动作。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed as AI agents frequently exhibit user specification-grounding failures, executing hallucinated, undesired actions to force a resolution rather than expressing uncertainty. Existing detection methods fail to provide actionable, real-time correction as they either do not localize the hallucinations, or incur prohibitive inference latency. We introduce the Latent Critic, a lightweight low-rank adapter (LoRA) that operates concurrently with a frozen base LLM's generation to actively restructure the transformer's residual stream---amplifying latent grounding signals and translating them into localized, natural language feedback within a single sequence. By refining the base model's native uncertainty signals, this manipulation of the latent space enables reliable, granular detection without the overhead of secondary inference loops. Mechanistic analysis via activation patching and layer-wise probing shows that this rank-invariant behavior restructures pre-existing uncertainty geometry into a linearly separable representation that transfers more reliably than base model representations alone. Using tool-calling as an instantiation of granular hallucinations, we validate the detection and downstream improvements enabled by the Latent Critic architecture across Qwen and Llama-based models. Demonstrating superior real-time efficacy, our approach significantly outperforms equivalent-scale fine-tuned external detectors, semantic entropy baselines, and passive internal probes in isolating hallucinations, achieving 0.966 AUROC and >80% accuracy in localization (e.g., ungrounded: date). When deployed in a closed-loop ReAct environment, the Critic acts as a negligible latency guardrail, intercepting hallucinations before execution to prevent undesired actions while simultaneously leveraging this specific localized feedback to enable efficient agent self-correction.

</details>


### [145] [Do Time-Series Forecasters Use the Right History: Recoverability, Recovery, and Functional Use of Temporal Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10433)
*Qipeng Qian,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，即便预测精度很高且延迟报告正确，也无法证明模型真正使用了正确的历史输入。作者提出了输入条件化的可恢复性度量，证明延迟报告可以非常可靠而预测风险接近最优，但模型仍使用错误的滞后；并通过实验和路由控制方法验证了这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测精度本身无法回答"哪些历史输入产生了预测"这一根本问题。现有研究较少系统地分离"真实延迟能否从数据中恢复"、"模型是否报告了正确延迟"以及"预测是否实际使用了正确历史"这三个不同的问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者首先将问题拆解为三个独立问题，提出输入条件化的可恢复性度量以区分内在模糊性与模型误差；随后通过理论证明构造了延迟报告可靠但预测使用错误滞后的情形；在有限样本上对N-HiTS和TCN模型进行了匹配遮蔽测试；最后提出通过将预测路由到报告历史的方式（硬one-hot控制）来消除旁路路径。

Result: 在点延迟任务中，延迟报告正确且归一化超额风险在最优10%以内的预测中，N-HiTS有55.4%的案例其报告历史在遮蔽测试下是功能上未使用的，TCN则为92.7%。路由控制方法能够实现精确的固定报告对齐。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：即便预测准确且延迟报告正确，也不能证明模型使用了正确的历史输入。<br>论文以"本文内容未通过合规审查，已隐藏"回应。实际上，这个结论对时间序列模型的可解释性和诊断方法提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Forecast accuracy does not tell us which past inputs produced a prediction. We separate three questions for time-series models with known delay structure: can the true delay be recovered from the observed data, does the model report it, and does the forecast actually use the same history? We first derive input-conditioned recoverability measures that separate intrinsic ambiguity from model error. We then prove that a delay report can become arbitrarily reliable while forecast risk approaches the oracle even though the predictor still uses the wrong lag. This failure also appears in finite samples on the point-delay task: among forecasts with a correct delay report and normalized excess risk within 10\% of the oracle, the reported history is functionally unused under our matched masking test in 55.4\% of N-HiTS cases and 92.7\% of TCN cases. Finally, we show that routing the prediction through the reported history removes off-report bypass paths; a hard one-hot control achieves exact fixed-report alignment. The main conclusion is simple: a good forecast, even with a correct delay report, does not show that the model used the right history.

</details>


### [146] [Detecting an Effect Is Not Learning to Act on It: A Reward-SNR Floor for LLM Acquisition Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10441)
*Ying Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关键区分：检测辅助信号（如LLM推理、慢速oracle）在平均意义上有效≠学习在单个实例上何时使用它。作者推导出奖励信噪比（reward-SNR）的检测下限rho*(N)≈2.8/sqrt(N)，低于该下限时，即使oracle显示可观的表观增益，任何可部署的策略都无法学习何时获取信号——学习到的路由不会优于随机。作者引入Structured Hypothesis Embeddings (SHE)作为具体实例，在三个公开数据集（MIND, REES46, Amazon-Beauty）上验证：SHE虽忠实且可校准，但全部数据集均低于SNR下限，因此实际可实现的是设计时的"regime gate"而非逐实例策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多pipeline可以花费一定代价获取模型驱动的辅助观测（如LLM的结构化推理、慢速oracle、昂贵测量），但核心问题是：何时值得使用这些信号？作者指出一个容易被忽视的区别——在平均意义上检测到信号有帮助，与学习如何在每个实例上使用它，是两件完全不同的事。存在一个reward-SNR的下限阈值，决定后者是否可能实现。

Method: 作者通过跨per-impression、cluster、regime、uplift-tree等多种粒度进行经验验证，证明当数据集低于SNR下限时，学习路由始终不优于随机，且matched-moment噪声placebo可复现oracle表观增益的>=100%（即表观"可学习结构"只是噪声的次序统计量）。理论层面推导了可估计性边界rho*(N)≈2.8/sqrt(N)。具体实例上，引入SHE（用冻结LLM将用户历史转化为排序的、带置信度的、有证据支撑的意图假设，融合进推荐器），在三个公开数据集上验证其忠实性、可校准性及值的有条件性。

Result: 即使信号忠实且oracle显示显著表观增益，在四个不同粒度上学习的路由均不优于随机；噪声placebo能够复现>=100%的oracle增益，证明表观可学习性只是噪声伪影。理论推导的SNR下限为rho*(N)≈2.8/sqrt(N)，通过正对照确认这是真实的低SNR极限而非pipeline缺陷。SHE在有序GRU上显著增益+0.0114（95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209]），但全局冗余间隙与零无法区分，且三个数据集均低于SNR下限导致学习型获取在任何粒度上都失效。

Conclusion: 可实现的单元是设计时的regime gate（判断某个数据集/骨干网络是否高于SNR下限），而非逐实例的获取策略。当数据集低于reward-SNR下限时，任何尝试学习逐实例路由的做法都是无效的。作者发布代码和一条命令复现。

Abstract: Many pipelines can pay a per-example cost to acquire an auxiliary, model-derived observation -- an LLM's structured reasoning, a slow oracle, an expensive measurement -- and then must decide when the acquired signal is worth using. Our thesis is a distinction that is easy to miss: detecting that such a signal helps on average is not the same as learning to act on it per instance, and a reward-SNR floor governs when the second is even possible. Even when the signal is faithful and an in-sample oracle picking the top-b examples by realized reward shows a sizable apparent gain, no deployable policy can learn when to acquire it: across per-impression, cluster, regime, and uplift-tree granularities, learned routing never beats random, and a matched-moment noise placebo reproduces >=100% of the oracle's apparent gain -- the apparent "learnable structure" is order statistics of noise. We explain this with one distinction, detecting a mean effect vs. learning a per-instance acquisition policy, and a reward-SNR detectability floor: routing is estimable offline only if the reward SNR rho clears rho*(N) ~= 2.8/sqrt(N), with a positive control confirming a true low-SNR limit rather than a broken pipeline. As a concrete instantiation we introduce Structured Hypothesis Embeddings (SHE): a frozen LLM turns a user history into ranked, confidence-scored, evidence-grounded intent hypotheses, fused into a recommender. On three public datasets (MIND, REES46, Amazon-Beauty), SHE is faithful and calibratable, yet its value is backbone- and regime-conditional (significant over an ordered GRU, +0.0114, 95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209], but a global redundancy gap indistinguishable from zero), and learned acquisition collapses at every granularity because all three datasets sit below the floor. The realizable unit is a design-time regime gate, not a per-instance policy. We release code and a one-command reproduction.

</details>


### [147] [A Joint-Distribution Route to Fair Representations with Continuous Sensitive Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10470)
*Yijin Ni,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过联合差异d(P_{Z,S}, P_Z⊗P_S)来评估连续敏感属性下的公平表示学习独立性，取代传统需要逐值条件律估计的方法。以HSIC作为联合差异实例，证明其与条件MMD积分等价（仅差一个显式谱尾部），并在统计效率上优于条件路径估计器（O(n^{-1/2}) vs O(n^{-2/5})），同时实现了可比的公平-准确率权衡和更低的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的公平表示学习方法（如广义人口均等、EIPM、互信息）在处理连续敏感属性时，需要对每个敏感值s估计条件律P_{Z|S=s}与边际P_Z之间的差异，这需要非参数条件平滑，统计效率低下且计算代价高。

Method: 提出用单一联合差异d(P_{Z,S}, P_Z⊗P_S)替代条件积分形式，建立了分解恒等式（disintegration identity），证明在可分解见证类上与条件积分泛函等价。以HSIC作为联合差异的具体实例，直接从样本估计而不需要条件平滑，推导其与条件MMD积分之间的谱尾部关系，并实现FRHSIC算法。

Result: HSIC估计器是闭式O(n²)统计量，收敛速度为O(n^{-1/2})，而条件路径估计器仅达到非参数的O(n^{-2/5})速率。FRHSIC算法在公平-准确率权衡上与条件路径基线相当，但显著降低了每轮训练时间。

Conclusion: 通过联合差异的视角，可以在不显式估计条件律的情况下达到与条件积分相同的公平性目标，同时获得更优的统计效率和计算效率，为连续敏感属性的公平表示学习提供了更实用的框架。

Abstract: Fair representation learning with a continuous sensitive attribute $S$ requires a representation $Z$ that is statistically independent of $S$. Existing criteria, including generalized demographic parity, the expectation of integral probability metrics (EIPM), and mutual information, enforce this independence by averaging a per-value discrepancy between the conditional law $P_{Z \mid S=s}$ and the marginal $P_Z$ over the law of $S$. This approach requires a nonparametric surrogate for the conditional law at each sensitive value. We propose evaluating independence through a single joint discrepancy $d\left(P_{Z, S}, P_Z \otimes P_S\right)$ between the joint law and the product of its marginals. We establish a disintegration identity; on decomposable witness classes it equals the conditional-integral functional that EIPM and generalized demographic parity instantiate. By reaching the same target without the conditional law, this discrepancy can be estimated directly from samples via a dependence statistic rather than conditional smoothing. We take the Hilbert-Schmidt independence criterion (HSIC) as an instance of the joint discrepancy $d$ to investigate the statistical efficiency of replacing the conditional formulation. The HSIC estimator is a closed-form $O\left(n^2\right)$ statistic that converges at the $O\left(n^{-1 / 2}\right)$ rate, in contrast to the nonparametric $O\left(n^{-2 / 5}\right)$ rate of the conditional-route estimators. We prove this instance is equivalent to the conditional maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) integral up to an explicit spectral tail. The corresponding algorithmic implementation, i.e., FRHSIC, attains fairness-accuracy tradeoffs comparable to conditional-route basel es while reducing per-epoch training time.

</details>


### [148] [Critic-Free Pretraining for Efficient Online Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10473)
*Daoyi Li,Yixian Zhang,Chao Yu,Wenbo Ding,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Critic-Free Pretraining (CFP)，一种放弃离线训练critic的O2O强化学习范式，避免了离线critic估值偏差在在线微调阶段带来的负面影响，实验表明其能匹配或超越传统O2O算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离线-在线（O2O）强化学习方法直接复用离线训练的critic，但在线微调时策略和数据分布快速变化，导致离线继承的价值估计与在线环境不匹配，从而产生偏差并阻碍策略提升和探索效率。

Method: 提出Critic-Free Pretraining (CFP)范式，完全放弃离线critic训练，仅利用离线数据预训练策略，使得在线阶段新初始化的critic能够无偏地适应在线环境，且该范式与多种主流O2O算法兼容。

Result: CFP在多个多样化任务上与传统的O2O算法相比，能够一致地匹配或超越其性能，在若干具有挑战性的任务上获得了尤其显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 离线critic训练并非O2O强化学习的必要环节，放弃离线critic训练可以避免估值偏差的继承，从而更高效地进行在线微调，为O2O强化学习提供了一种更简洁有效的范式。

Abstract: Offline-to-online (O2O) reinforcement learning aims to leverage policies pretrained on static datasets while improving them through online interaction. However, directly reusing an offline-trained critic can hinder online fine-tuning: as the policy and data distribution change rapidly, value estimates inherited from offline training may become misaligned with the online environment, leading to inaccurate policy improvement and inefficient exploration. To address this problem, we introduce \textbf{C}ritic-\textbf{F}ree \textbf{P}retraining: an efficient paradigm that completely abandons the approach of offline critic training, allowing a freshly initialized critic to adapt without inheriting biased estimates. CFP is compatible with various mainstream O2O algorithms and consistently matches or improves upon conventional O2O algorithms across a diverse set of tasks, with particularly pronounced gains on several challenging tasks.

</details>


### [149] [Exploration-Driven Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Intrinsic Motivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10499)
*Md Rafid Islam,Rafsan Jany,Zahid Hasan,Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于内在动机的探索驱动个性化联邦强化学习框架（EDPFRL-IM），通过在每个客户端引入基于好奇心的探索机制和随机网络蒸馏（RND）信号来增强本地探索，同时服务器仅收集最小新颖性摘要而非原始数据，以保护客户端隐私，在稀疏奖励环境中显著提升了策略个性化和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦强化学习（PFRL）方法过度依赖现有的强化学习奖励信号来推导每个客户端的最优策略，忽视了在非平稳或稀疏奖励环境中的探索问题，导致策略发现受限。

Method: 提出EDPFRL-IM框架：1）每个客户端引入基于好奇心的内在动机探索机制，促进本地探索并保护客户端隐私；2）客户端向外部奖励中叠加随机网络蒸馏（RND）信号，以帮助在先前未探索的状态空间中促进策略发现；3）服务器不访问客户端的原始经验或本地梯度估计，而是发送全局探索先验并收集来自每个客户端的最小新颖性摘要，以实现客户端之间的多样化和协调探索。

Result: 在基准环境中的实验表明，该框架在策略个性化和样本效率方面优于平均PFRL基准方法，尤其是在延迟和稀疏奖励系统中表现突出。

Conclusion: EDPFRL-IM将灵活的探索学习结构集成到联邦强化学习系统中，同时保护客户端隐私，为个性化联邦强化学习在稀疏奖励环境中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning (PFRL) takes a decentralized approach to storing and accessing information based on past experiences while keeping each client's data private during the learning of each client's policy. Many current methods for PFRL rely heavily on exploiting existing reinforcement learning reward signals to derive an optimal policy for each client, thereby neglecting exploration in non-stationary or sparse-reward environments. In this work, we introduce a new exploration-driven framework, Exploration-Driven Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Intrinsic Motivation (EDPFRL-IM), that leverages an inherent curiosity-driven exploration at each client to promote local exploration and protect client privacy. Furthermore, to facilitate policy discovery via exploration in previously unexplored state spaces, clients add an intrinsic random network distillation (RND) signal to their extrinsic reward. Additionally, the server does not have access to clients' raw experiences or local gradient estimates; instead, the server sends global exploration priors and collects minimal novelty summaries from each client to enable both diverse and coordinated exploration among clients. Experiments in benchmark environments show that our framework outperforms average PFRL benchmarks in policy personalization and sample efficiency, primarily in delayed and sparse reward systems. Overall, EDPFRL-IM enables the integration of a flexible exploratory learning structure into federated reinforcement learning systems while preserving client privacy.

</details>


### [150] [Coordinating the Unknown Lipschitz Constant in Multiplayer Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10526)
*Ricardo Parada,Chenzhang Zhao,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究连续（Lipschitz）动作空间下的合作式多智能体bandits问题，其中Lipschitz常数未知。针对三种信息结构（共同奖励但动作不可观测、动作可观测但奖励独立、两者均不可观测），设计了能够估计Lipschitz常数、选择联合动作空间离散化并应用合作bandit方法的算法。核心难点在于智能体无法通信时必须从各自数据达成相同的离散化方案，论文通过证明表明共同奖励和可观测动作可以免费提供这种一致性，而在两者都缺失时，可通过抖动量化以不牺牲主导阶遗憾的代价实现一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化应用（如分布式优化、联邦学习、多智能体系统）中，多个智能体需要协作探索未知环境，但实际场景中动作空间通常连续且环境的平滑性（Lipschitz常数）未知，这给多智能体协同学习带来了新的挑战。

Method: 设计了估计Lipschitz常数的机制，基于估计结果选择联合动作空间的离散化方案，并将合作式bandit方法应用于诱导出的离散问题。对于无法通信的智能体，采用抖动量化（dithered quantization）技术确保所有智能体能从各自数据中收敛到相同的离散化方案。

Result: 证明了遗憾上界，表明共同奖励和可观测动作各自都能"免费"提供离散化一致性；而在两者均不可观测的情况下，抖动量化仍然可以以不增加主导阶遗憾为代价实现一致性。

Conclusion: 在连续动作空间且Lipschitz常数未知的合作式多智能体bandits问题中，不同的信息结构对达成一致的离散化方案有不同的影响，但即便在最困难的信息结构下，也能通过在估计上施加抖动量化实现协作，且不会付出主导阶的遗憾代价。

Abstract: Motivated by decentralized applications, we study cooperative multi-agent bandits in continuous (Lipschitz) action spaces when the Lipschitz constant is unknown. We consider three information structures: (A)~unobserved actions with common rewards, (B)~observed actions with independent rewards, and (C)~unobserved actions with independent rewards. In each case we design and analyze an algorithm that estimates the Lipschitz constant, chooses a discretization of the joint action space, and applies a cooperative bandit method to the induced discrete problem. Players never communicate once learning starts, so the central difficulty is that they must reach the \emph{same} discretization from their own data. We prove regret guarantees showing that common rewards and observable actions each supply this agreement for free, and that in their absence agreement can still be bought, through a dithered quantization of the estimate, at no cost in the leading order of the regret.

</details>


### [151] [Robust Multi-Agent Bandits with Heavy-Tailed Rewards and Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10529)
*Daphne Feng,Ricardo Parada,Lily Jiang,Sophia Yi,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重尾奖励分布下多智能体多臂老虎机问题，针对三种信息不对称场景（动作不可观测/奖励共享、动作可观测/奖励独立、动作不可观测/奖励独立）分别提出了鲁棒的分布式算法，其遗憾界几乎匹配集中式重尾老虎机的理论速率，并在Pareto分布环境上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多臂老虎机研究大多假设次高斯奖励，但现实应用中常出现重尾分布且智能体间存在信息不对称。现有方法无法同时处理重尾奖励和去中心化交互，亟需针对不同信息不对称场景设计鲁棒算法。

Method: 针对三种信息不对称机制（公共奖励下的不可观测动作、独立奖励下的可观测动作、独立奖励下的不可观测动作），分别设计了鲁棒的分布式多智能体老虎机算法，利用重尾鲁棒估计技术结合多智能体协调/同步策略。

Result: 所提算法在三种信息不对称场景下的遗憾界均几乎匹配集中式重尾老虎机的下界/最优速率；在Pareto分布奖励环境上的实验验证了理论分析，并展示了同步、协调与探索之间在不同机制下的权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作将重尾奖励的鲁棒性保证拓展到多智能体去中心化场景，系统性地揭示了信息不对称程度如何影响算法性能与探索-协调权衡，为实际重尾环境下的分布式决策提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The multi-armed bandit problem is a central framework in sequential decision-making, extensively studied under sub-Gaussian reward assumptions. However, real-world applications often involve heavy-tailed reward distributions and decentralized, information-asymmetric interactions. We study multi-agent multi-armed bandits with heavy-tailed rewards under three information-asymmetry regimes: unobserved actions with common rewards, observed actions with independent rewards, and unobserved actions with independent rewards. We develop robust decentralized algorithms for each setting and derive regret guarantees that nearly match centralized heavy-tailed rates. Experiments on a Pareto-distributed reward environment validate our theoretical findings and illustrate the trade-offs between synchronization, coordination, and exploration across the three regimes.

</details>


### [152] [Retrieval-Corrected Conformal Prediction for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10553)
*Sangjin Jin,Kangmin Kim,Junhyeong Lee,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出检索-校正共形预测（RCCP），一种用于时间序列预测区间的不确定性量化方法。RCCP通过检索相似历史残差作为局部证据，并利用标量共形校正来修正检索后的覆盖率误差，从而构建预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测的校准过程对时间序列数据效率较低，因为预测误差具有时间依赖性和非平稳性。现有时间序列CP方法虽通过局部校准改进，但残差加权或额外适应过程可能稀释当前预测最相关的证据，导致局部校准不够直接。

Method: RCCP从检索到的单侧残差构建不对称区间，并用标量共形校正来校准归一化检索误差。检索提供局部残差证据，共形校正确定最终所需的覆盖尺度，二者互补。

Result: 在标准基准和多种骨干预测器上，RCCP在所有设置中均达到目标覆盖率，并取得最低的Winkler分数和更少的严重未命中。同时计算和校准开销低，具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 检索-校正校准是时间序列预测中不确定性量化的一种有效且可扩展的方法，证明了检索增强共形校准的实用性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides distribution-free prediction intervals for fixed forecasters, but its standard calibration procedure is often inefficient for time series data, where forecast errors are temporally dependent and change across time and operating conditions. Recent time series CP methods improve local calibration using recent, weighted, or localized residuals. Yet local calibration can remain indirect, since broad residual weighting or additional adaptation procedures may dilute the evidence most relevant to the current prediction. This motivates a simple retrieval and correction strategy that selects similar past residuals as local evidence and then corrects the coverage error left by retrieval. In this paper, we propose Retrieval--Corrected Conformal Prediction (RCCP), a retrieval-augmented calibration method for time series prediction intervals. RCCP builds an asymmetric interval from retrieved one-sided residuals and calibrates its normalized retrieval error with a scalar conformal correction. Thus, retrieval provides local residual evidence, while conformal correction determines the final scale needed for coverage. We provide a coverage-gap bound based on the stability of the normalized retrieval error distribution. Across standard benchmarks and backbone forecasters, RCCP attains the target coverage in every setting and achieves the lowest Winkler scores, with fewer severe misses. RCCP also achieves low calibration and inference overhead, showing that retrieval-corrected calibration is an effective and scalable approach to uncertainty quantification in time series forecasting. Code is available at https://github.com/jinsaaang/rccp.

</details>


### [153] [MARCO: Click-Intent Decomposition for Calibrated Ads Conversion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10562)
*Shiwen Shen,Xiru Huang,Liang Luo,Jianbo Sun,He Lyu,Zihang Fu,Ivonne Xu,Zhizhuo Li,Zhengyu Zhang,Pei-Ju Sung,Yunmiao Wang,Zixuan Wang,Zhengli Zhao,Qiang Jin,Mike Jermann,Mingda Li,Yang Xiao,Bhavana Challa,Brooke Bian,Yang Li,Ashish Chamoli,Bibek Bhusal,Danning Di,Yuan Jin,Meet Raval,Zhiwen Chen,Boyao Sun,Shuguang Wang,Yunlong He,Yantao Yao,Sagar Chordia,Wenlin Chen,Santanu Kolay,Qin Huang,Ellie Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 工业广告排序中，不同点击类型反映不同的用户意图，但标准CVR模型将所有点击视为等同，导致高意图点击被低估、低意图点击被高估，尽管整体校准看似近乎完美。作者提出MARCO框架，利用点击类型作为免费的行为标签，按意图拆解点击训练独立的CVR头，在线上组合各意图的CVR估计，理论证明拆解不会增加总体风险，实际部署中纠正了各意图的校准至约100%，每次点击转化率提升+2.80%，总指标累计提升+0.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准广告排序将转化概率拆分为CTR和CVR，但将每次点击视为相同事件。实际上，用户通过物理UI交互提供了免费的意图信号，同一广告的不同点击类型在实际转化率上存在4倍差异。将它们混为一谈会在聚合校准看似完美的情况下掩盖系统性的估计偏差。

Method: 提出MARCO框架：1）使用日志中的点击类型作为免费行为标签，训练每个意图独立的CVR头，在异构群体上训练；2）在服务时，根据预测的意图分布组合各意图的CVR估计；3）理论上证明分解不会提高总体风险，并给出平方损失下的精确提升空间和部署损失下的非负性；4）通过路由效率拨盘控制多少提升能到达服务端；5）将多展示、多点击归因建模为RL回报估计类似的信用分配问题，提出低偏差低方差的确定性归因方案，并推导了三个端到端一致性条件。

Result: 在二元意图粒度部署下，MARCO将各意图的校准纠正至约100%，每次点击转化率提升+2.80%，总体业务指标累计改善+0.98%。离线与在线实验均验证了收益来源于有限容量下的估计与校准效应，而非改变总体最优打分。

Conclusion: 通过识别并利用用户UI交互中的免费意图信号，MARCO证明了点击意图拆解能够在不改变总体最优得分的前提下，通过改善有限容量下的估计与校准来释放真实收益，为工业广告排序提供了理论与实践兼备的改进框架。

Abstract: Not all clicks are equal. Industrial ads ranking decouples conversion probability into click-through rate (CTR) and post-click conversion rate (CVR), yet treats every click as the same event. In reality, users provide a free, self-generated signal of intent through their physical UI interactions. Different click types on the same ad exhibit a 4-fold difference in actual conversion rates. By conflating these signals, the standard CVR model under-predicts high-intent clicks and over-predicts low-intent ones, which is a bias masked by near-perfect aggregate calibration. We propose MARCO (Multi-intent Ads Ranking Composition Optimization), a framework that resolves this bias by decomposing each click by intent. Using the logged click type as a free behavioral label, MARCO trains per-intent CVR heads on homogeneous populations, and at serving time composes their per-intent CVR estimates under a predicted distribution over intents. Theoretically, we prove that decomposition never raises population risk, give the exact headroom under squared loss and non-negativity under the deployed loss, and show through a routing-efficiency dial how much of it reaches serving. Because the population-optimal score is unchanged, any gain is a finite-capacity estimation and calibration effect that we validated both offline and online. For deployment at scale, we further cast multi-impression, multi-click attribution as credit assignment with a bias-variance tradeoff analogous to RL return estimation, showing last-impression, first-click attribution is the low-bias, low-variance, deterministic choice under production constraints, and derive three consistency conditions enforced end-to-end at scale. Deployed at binary intent granularity, MARCO corrects per-intent calibration to approximately 100%, lifts conversions per click by +2.80%, and drives +0.98% cumulative improvement in topline metrics.

</details>


### [154] [BREAD: Baseline-Referenced Explanations for Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10587)
*Jiaqi Qiu,Rob Goedhart,Jannis Kurtz,Inez M. Zwetsloot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的、基于基线参照的诊断方法，用于AI驱动的预测性异常检测，在均值偏移异常设定下比LIME具有更高的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于AI的统计过程监控(SPM)在检测到异常后，需要诊断方法识别导致异常的变量。传统SPM诊断方法针对特定检测模型设计，无法直接应用于AI方法。模型无关的可解释AI(XAI)虽提供通用框架，但存在可扩展性限制或会将相关性分配给噪声特征的问题，降低诊断准确性。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的、基线参照的诊断方法，同时利用异常观测和正常基线信息。在均值偏移异常设定下提供数学保证，证明所提方法在识别造成异常的变量方面比LIME具有更高的忠实度。

Result: 通过仿真研究和真实案例研究验证了所提方法的有效性，结果表明该方法能够为基于AI的预测性异常检测方法生成更忠实、更准确的诊断结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的基线参照诊断方法有效克服了现有XAI方法在异常诊断中的局限性，为AI驱动的SPM提供了更准确、可扩展的诊断工具。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based prospective anomaly detection methods are increasingly deployed in high-dimensional and nonlinear settings. Among these approaches, AI-based statistical process monitoring (SPM) is widely used, providing a structured framework for prospective monitoring. Once an anomaly is detected, a diagnosis method is needed to identify the features driving the flagged observation away from normal behaviour. Traditional SPM diagnosis methods are typically designed for specific detection models and cannot be directly applied to AI-based methods. Model-agnostic explainable AI (XAI) offers a general framework for feature relevance explanation. However, existing methods suffer from scalability limitations or assign relevance to noise features, reducing diagnosis accuracy. We propose a scalable, baseline-referenced diagnosis method that uses both the anomalous observation and normal baseline information. We provide mathematical guarantees that under a mean-shift anomaly setting, the proposed method achieves higher faithfulness in detecting the features causing the anomaly compared to LIME. Simulation studies and a real-world case study validate the effectiveness of the proposed method and show that it generates more faithful and accurate diagnosis results for AI-based prospective anomaly detection methods.

</details>


### [155] [$β$-VAEs as Effective Theories: Tolerance-Dependent Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10599)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究β-VAE在非线性全连接网络下的谱截断行为，发现尽管非线性相互作用使坍缩阈值与重建效用不再精确对齐，但排序关系在已解析的秩上依然保持，谱截断仍可作为效用截断使用，同时揭示深度增加带来"头部集中、尾部劣化"的有效维度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性高斯VAE中，β正则化强度增加会按照PCA谱精确排序地坍缩低效用潜变量坐标。作者想探究这一性质在非线性全连接VAE（以WorldClim数据训练）中有多少能够保留下来。

Method: 在非线性全连接VAE上训练WorldClim数据，对比β-VAE正则化强度增加时的潜变量坍缩顺序与线性高斯情形的差异，分析坍缩阈值与重建效用之间的对应关系，并绘制有效维度曲线。

Result: 非线性相互作用使坍缩起始点发生偏移和展宽，阈值不再与效用精确重合；但在已解析的秩范围内排序仍然保持，谱截断仍等价于效用截断。有效维度曲线显示深度增加会在前几个坐标集中效用但牺牲尾部保真度。

Conclusion: 谱截断作为效用截断的逻辑在非线性VAE中依然成立，有效描述框架可继续沿用；但深度选择需要在头部效用集中与尾部保真之间进行权衡。

Abstract: In a $β$-VAE, increasing the regularization strength acts as a spectral cutoff by collapsing low-utility latent coordinates.
  In the linear Gaussian VAE, the collapse order matches the ranking of reconstruction utilities exactly, because both are set by the PCA spectrum. We ask which parts of this picture survive in fully connected nonlinear VAEs trained on WorldClim.
  We find that nonlinear interactions shift and broaden collapse onsets, so thresholds no longer coincide exactly with utilities. However, the common ordering is preserved over the resolved ranks, so the spectral cutoff still acts as a utility cutoff and the effective-description logic carries through.
  The resulting effective-dimension curves reveal a head--tail tradeoff: increasing depth concentrates utility into the first few coordinates but worsens tail fidelity.

</details>


### [156] [Compute-Optimal Is Not Cluster-Optimal: Systems-Aware Scaling for Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10605)
*Soumajyoti Sarkar,Yuxin Tang,Sheng Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MOSAIC框架，将模型架构与系统协同设计形式化为优化问题，通过耦合预测性缩放定律与校准的性能模型（估计MFU、通信成本、内存占用和最佳并行布局），应用于稀疏混合专家（MoE）语言模型。研究发现，在仅考虑计算（FLOPs）预算时，稀疏度不存在内部最优解；而考虑集群系统约束后，最优稀疏度才会出现，论证了架构与系统应统一协同设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练中，算法、架构和系统决策通常分阶段独立进行：先通过缩放定律选择架构和训练方案，再单独优化硬件效率的系统实现。这种割裂的方式忽略了架构选择与系统约束之间的相互影响，可能导致次优的整体设计。作者希望打破这种分离，将两者统一为协同优化问题。

Method: MOSAIC将架构与系统协同设计建模为优化问题，核心包含两部分：(1) 预测性缩放定律，覆盖稀疏MoE模型，缩放维度包括稀疏因子（每token前向传播中非激活参数的比例），模型规模从1.04亿到27亿激活参数、总参数达790亿；(2) 校准的性能模型，用于估计MFU、通信成本、内存占用及最佳并行布局。通过耦合两者在给定集群约束下搜索最优架构配置。

Result: 在仅考虑FLOPs预算时，效率无关的模型计算预算内不存在内部最优稀疏度——损失随模型稀疏度增加单调下降，计算最优值位于数据支撑的上边界。然而，当引入集群的系统约束（通过MOSAIC捕获）后，MoE模型的最优稀疏度才会真正出现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，仅以模型FLOPs为目标的缩放定律选择会倾向于最大化稀疏度，但这并不符合实际系统效率最优。最优架构高度依赖集群系统条件，这强烈支持前沿语言模型训练应转向统一的架构与系统协同设计（co-design）范式。

Abstract: In large-scale pretraining, the algorithm, architecture, and systems decisions are conventionally made in disconnected stages. A scaling law stage selects an architecture and training recipe, optimizing loss under compute constraints, and a separate systems stage then optimizes the implementation for hardware efficiency. In this work, we develop MOSAIC, which formulates model architecture and systems co-design as an optimization problem. MOSAIC couples a predictive scaling law with a calibrated performance model that estimates Model FLOPs Utilization (MFU), communication cost, memory footprint, and the best parallel layout. We instantiate the framework for sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models, where expert count, routing sparsity, and other MoE layer dimensions affect both the loss and systems efficiency. We fit a scaling law on sparse MoE models trained on text data, whose scaling dimensions include the sparsity factor, which is the fraction of model parameters inactive per token in a forward pass. The scaling law sweeps in our work span active parameters from $104$ million to $2.7$ billion and total model sizes reaching $79$ billion parameters. We show that, within the calibrated sparsity range, an efficiency-agnostic model-FLOPs budget admits no interior optimal sparsity. The fitted loss decreases monotonically with sparser models and the compute optimum lies at the upper boundary of the data support. An optimal sparsity in MoE models instead emerges under the cluster's systems constraints, as captured by MOSAIC. Our results argue for a shift towards unified architecture and systems co-design for frontier language model training.

</details>


### [157] [Pair-Centric Graph Rewiring for Over-Squashing via Optimal Transport-Guided Communication Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10619)
*Yan Wang,Chuan-Xian Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PairAlign，一个以节点对为中心的图重连框架，通过"需求-支持短缺"评分显式识别需要结构支持的节点对通信，并利用最优传输指导的机制在有限重连预算下协调分配边，以缓解消息传递神经网络中的过压缩问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消息传递神经网络在处理图上远距离分布的任务相关信息时，因局部传播需通过有限结构接口压缩远程信号而表现不佳。现有图重连方法多依赖边级瓶颈分数或图级连通性替代指标，未明确回答在有限重连预算下哪些节点对通信最需要结构支持这一关键问题。

Method: PairAlign将原始图的结构需求与当前图的有限跳传播支持相结合，通过两者的比率突出通信需求未被拓扑支持的交互；理论证明该分数可作为基于Jacobian的短缺的可计算代理。同时揭示边添加的双面效应（新增有效游走路径同时稀释现有归一化转移质量），并引入最优传输引导的重连机制，在候选边预算与短缺目标之间协调通信对齐，实现更广泛有效的短缺目标覆盖。

Result: 在标准图基准上的实验表明，PairAlign在多种消息传递骨干网络上均带来改进，验证了节点对级别的修复作为缓解过压缩的有效途径。

Conclusion: PairAlign通过节点对中心的重连框架，将过压缩问题从边级/G图级视角提升到对级视角，结合理论驱动的短缺评分与最优传输预算分配，为有限预算下的图重连提供了更精准且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) often struggle when task-relevant information is distributed across distant regions of a graph, since local propagation must compress remote signals through limited structural interfaces. Graph rewiring provides a structural response to over-squashing. Most existing methods rely on edge-level bottleneck scores or graph-level connectivity surrogates. With a limited rewiring budget, the key question is which pairwise communications most need structural support. This paper proposes PairAlign, a pair-centric graph rewiring framework that makes this question explicit through demand-support shortage. Specifically, PairAlign combines original-graph structural demand with current-graph finite-hop propagation support; their ratio highlights interactions whose communication demand is poorly supported by topology, and our theory shows that this score provides a computable proxy for the corresponding Jacobian-based shortage with a pair-level interpretation of over-squashing. Our theory reveals a two-sided effect of edge insertion: a new edge can create useful walks and simultaneously dilute existing normalized transition mass. Guided by this observation, PairAlign optimizes shortage to favor edge additions that alleviate over-squashing. Beyond selecting useful additions, PairAlign further introduces an Optimal Transport-guided rewiring mechanism to coordinate the finite edge budget for pair-level structural compatibility and shortage-target coverage. It formulates communication alignment between the candidate edge budget and the shortage targets, and the theory shows that this allocation covers shortage targets more broadly and effectively than a greedy-local assignment. Experiments on standard graph benchmarks show PairAlign's improvement across message-passing backbones, validating pair-level repair as an effective route for alleviating over-squashing.

</details>


### [158] [ProbGuard: Calibrated Safety Risk Estimation from LLM Output Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10621)
*Xinzhe Huang,Biwu Yao,Kedong Xiu,Mengnan Zhao,Di Wang,Puning Zhao,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProbGuard，首个完全概率化、架构无关的LLM安全护栏，利用模型早期输出的分布信号来估计和校准安全概率，实现不安全输出的早期停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全护栏将安全评估建模为确定性分类任务，存在两个局限：一是安全评估本质具有不确定性，尤其是在生成早期阶段；二是仅依赖离散token序列，丢弃了LLM输出分布中丰富的概率信息。

Method: 给定LLM生成前缀的分布，将安全风险形式化为其继续生成动态的不安全概率，并通过蒙特卡洛采样估计该风险；随后在分布信号和已校准的安全风险上进行后训练。

Result: 在9个模型-数据集组合设置中取得最佳校准性能，相对于最优基线将平均Brier分数和ECE分别降低79.6%和71.9%；仅观察前10个解码步骤的早期输出分布，即可将6种代表性越狱攻击的成功率限制在至多1%。

Conclusion: ProbGuard通过利用LLM早期输出分布信号进行概率化的安全风险评估与校准，有效实现了不安全输出的早期拦截，显著优于传统确定性分类护栏。

Abstract: Recent research on Large Language Model (LLM) safety has widely adopted guardrails to identify unsafe LLM outputs. Existing guardrails typically formulate safety assessment as a deterministic classification task, mapping a discrete token sequence to a discrete safety label. However, this paradigm has two limitations: First, safety assessment is inherently an uncertain problem, particularly during the early generation state. Second, relying solely on discrete token sequences discards the rich probabilistic information embedded in the LLM output distribution. To address these limitations, we propose the first completely probabilistic architecture-agnostic guardrail \textsc{ProbGuard} to leverage the LLM early output distributional signals for estimating and calibrating the safety probability, thereby enabling early stopping of unsafe ongoing outputs. Specifically, given an LLM's generated prefix distribution, we formulate the safety risk as the unsafe probability of its continued generation dynamics and estimate this risk by Monte-Carlo sampling. Through post-training on the distributional signals and calibrated safety risk, \textsc{ProbGuard} achieves the best calibration performance across all nine model--dataset combination settings, reducing the average Brier score and ECE by 79.6\% and 71.9\%, respectively, over the best baseline. \textsc{ProbGuard} further limits the attack success rate to at most 1\% across six representative jailbreak attacks after observing the LLM early output distributions from only the first ten decoding steps.

</details>


### [159] [IADD-TR: Intervention-Aware Dynamics Decoupling with Targeted Regularization for Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10634)
*Zefeng Liang,Jie Qiao,Ruichu Cai,Weilin Chen,Zhifeng Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IADD-TR框架，通过干预感知动力学解耦和针对性正则化，解决基于模型的强化学习中策略诱导数据偏差导致的动作与环境演化纠缠以及反事实价值估计失真问题，在MuJoCo任务上取得竞争性回报和更优的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MBRL方法将转移模型和评论家视为整体预测器，忽略了策略诱导的数据偏差。这导致动作与环境演化纠缠在一起，且动作覆盖不均匀会扭曲用于策略改进的反事实价值估计，影响样本效率。

Method: IADD-TR框架由两部分组成：(1) IADD将转移分解为动作干预阶段和无动作自然演化阶段，使用零动作锚点解决分解的非唯一性，实现鲁棒泛化，其潜变量和状态对齐组件分别具有可辨识性；(2) TR基于重放状态策略梯度泛函的有效影响函数推导，为评论家增添动作密度缩放的残差校正项，并优化针对性损失函数，实现双重鲁棒的策略梯度估计。

Result: 在五个MuJoCo任务上的大量实验表明，IADD-TR取得了竞争性的回报，同时提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: IADD-TR通过解耦动作干预与环境自然演化的动力学建模，以及基于有效影响函数的双重鲁棒策略梯度估计，有效缓解了策略诱导数据偏差问题，为MBRL的样本效率提升提供了新的统一框架。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL), which learns environment dynamics to generate synthetic experience, is a promising approach to sample-efficient decision making. Numerous methods have been developed to improve dynamics prediction and policy optimization for MBRL through uncertainty estimation, model regularization, and conservative value learning. However, these methods typically treat the transition model and critic as monolithic predictors, overlooking the policy-induced data bias. Consequently, action can become entangled with environmental evolution, while uneven action coverage may distort the counterfactual value estimates used for policy improvement. To address this, we propose IADD-TR, a unified framework combining Intervention-Aware Dynamics Decoupling (IADD) and Targeted Regularization (TR). IADD factorizes transitions into an action-intervention stage and an action-free natural evolution stage, using a zero-action anchor to resolve the non-uniqueness of this two-stage factorization for robust generalization. Its latent and state-aligned components are identifiable up to an invertible within-block transformation and pointwise, respectively. For policy learning, we derive TR from the efficient influence function of a replay-state policy-gradient functional. TR augments the critic with an action-density-scaled residual correction and optimizes a targeted loss, yielding doubly robust policy-gradient estimation when either the critic or the replay action density is consistently specified. Extensive experiments on five MuJoCo tasks show that IADD-TR achieves competitive returns with improved sample efficiency.

</details>


### [160] [Optimize Cheap, Deploy Strong: Cost-Aware Cross-Tier Transfer for Evolutionary Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10694)
*Tal Oved,Roi Pony,Oshri Naparstek,Udi barzelay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种跨层级LLM提示词优化的成本控制方法，通过解耦LLM在搜索中扮演的三个角色（高容量回答、稀有反思/变异、目标部署），将大部分搜索token分配到最便宜的模型层级，在四个任务和十一个模型上实现与同层级优化相当的性能，同时将搜索成本降低5.6-54倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化优化的LLM提示词和智能体程序（如GEPA）主要成本瓶颈在于适应度评估——每个候选方案都需要在验证集上调用LLM进行评分，因此评估器的价格层级直接决定了总搜索成本。现有方法忽略了不同角色对模型能力的不同需求，导致成本高昂。

Method: 将LLM在搜索过程中扮演的三个角色解耦：高容量的回答角色使用最便宜的模型层级运行，强大的模型仅用于低频的反思/变体操作，并利用跨层级的正向迁移，将廉价层级进化出的提示词部署到更强的目标模型上。

Result: 在HotpotQA、IFBench、LiveBench-Math和HoVer四个任务上，使用四个模型家族的十一个模型进行验证，所得到的提示词与同层级优化性能相当，同时超过96%的搜索token被放置在最便宜的层级上，搜索成本降低5.6-14倍；在推理层级每次适应度调用都会产生长链思维的情况下，成本降低可达25-54倍。

Conclusion: 工作提供了一种成本可控的跨层级替代表征：明确了廉价层级搜索何时可以替代目标层级搜索，以及何时会失败，为低成本提示词进化搜索提供了新的实践路径。

Abstract: Evolutionary optimization of LLM prompts and agentic programs (e.g., GEPA) is dominated by fitness evaluation: scoring each candidate runs an answering LLM over a validation set, so the evaluator's price tier dictates total search cost. We restructure that search by decoupling the three roles an LLM plays, running the high-volume answering role on the cheapest tier, reserving a strong model for the rare reflection/variation operator, then exploiting upward cross-tier transfer to deploy the cheaply evolved prompt on a stronger target. We contribute a cost-controlled characterization of when cheap-tier search substitutes for target-tier search, and where it fails. Across four tasks (HotpotQA, IFBench, LiveBench-Math, HoVer) and eleven models in four model families, the resulting prompt matches or exceeds same-tier optimization while placing over 96% of search tokens on the cheapest tier, at 5.6-14x lower search cost, rising to 25-54x where reasoning tiers emit long chains of thought on every fitness call.

</details>


### [161] [ProTAGAD: A Foundation Model for TAG Anomaly Detection with Decoupled Topological and Textual Prototypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10699)
*Ziyan Wang,Liwen Wu,Cheng Xie,Song Gao,Zhenli He,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对文本属性图（TAG）异常检测中传统GNN模型因结构信息与文本语义深度耦合导致异常边界模糊（BAB问题）的挑战，提出了一种解耦拓扑和文本原型的基座模型。该框架构建双原型库，分别建模结构正常性和语义一致性，有效隔离被耦合聚合稀释的异常信号。在14个基准数据集上的跨域实验表明该方法达到最优性能，消融研究验证了BAB问题的普遍性及所提方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNN异常检测器在消息传递过程中不加区分地融合结构邻近性和文本语义，导致深度跨模态耦合。这种纠缠会放大噪声、模糊异常信号，引发Blurred-Anomaly-Boundary问题，使正常与异常的决策边界难以分离，且这一问题在图基座模型的跨域泛化中被进一步放大。

Method: 提出一种新型的TAG异常检测基座模型，核心设计是解耦拓扑原型库和文本原型库，独立建模结构正常性与语义一致性，避免耦合聚合对异常线索的稀释，从而有效隔离不同模态的异常信号。

Result: 在14个多样化基准数据集上的跨域实验中持续达到最先进性能；消融研究进一步验证了BAB问题在传统耦合检测器中的普遍存在，并证明了解耦原型设计能有效缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 解耦式双原型设计为文本属性图异常检测提供了一条有效路径，能够显著缓解跨模态耦合带来的异常边界模糊问题，并具备良好的跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs), endowed with abundant textual content along with topological structures, have emerged as a versatile backbone for real-world anomaly detection spanning large language model security, social network moderation, and cyber threat identification. Unlike conventional Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD), which relies primarily on structural irregularities, TAG anomaly detection must jointly leverage both topological patterns and fine-grained textual semantics to capture nuanced anomalous behaviors. The current GNN-based anomaly detectors adopt holistic message-passing schemes that indiscriminately fuse structural proximity and textual semantics during propagation, leading to deep cross-modality coupling. This entanglement acts as a noise amplifier, obscuring subtle anomalous signals and directly giving rise to the Blurred-Anomaly-Boundary (BAB) issue by rendering normal-anomalous decision boundaries poorly separable. This challenge is further amplified for graph foundation models that require robust cross-domain generalization. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel foundation model for TAG anomaly detection featuring decoupled topological and textual prototypes. Our framework constructs dual prototype banks to independently model structural normality and semantic consistency, effectively isolating anomaly cues that are otherwise diluted during coupled aggregation. Extensive experiments across 14 diverse benchmark datasets demonstrate that our method consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-domain settings. Notably, the ablation studies further corroborate the prevalence of the BAB issue in conventional coupled TAG anomaly detectors, and show that our decoupled prototype design effectively mitigates this challenge.

</details>


### [162] [SQuaT: Self-Supervised Knowledge Distillation via Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10709)
*HyeonJun Lee,Hyeonsik Jo,Jinwoo Chung,Jangho Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SQuaT框架，一种无标签的量化感知训练（QAT）方法，通过将教师特征用学生模型的量化参数进行量化来消除蒸馏损失中的不可达残差下界，从而在极端低比特（1-2比特）设置下取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将知识蒸馏（KD）与量化感知训练（QAT）结合的方法存在根本性局限：在蒸馏过程中，教师模型与学生量化模型之间的范围不匹配会产生不可达残差，导致蒸馏损失存在不可约的下界，限制了量化模型的性能上限。

Method: 提出SQuaT（Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features）框架，核心思想是在蒸馏过程中使用学生模型的量化参数来量化教师模型的特征，从理论上消除蒸馏损失的下界。该方法不依赖标签，适用于数据受限的实际场景。

Result: 在多种设置下的综合实验表明，SQuaT持续优于强基线方法，尤其在极端低比特（1比特和2比特）设置下增益显著。跨多种模型架构选择的大量评估显示该方法不依赖特定架构假设，具有广泛适用性。

Conclusion: SQuaT通过学生感知的教师特征量化策略，有效解决了QAT与KD结合时的范围不匹配问题，为无标签场景下的模型量化提供了理论上可证明的改进方案，并已开源代码便于复现。

Abstract: Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) enables the deployment of quantized models with minimal accuracy degradation. However, in practical scenarios, training labels are often unavailable due to privacy, copyright, or cost constraints. Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a common approach to address this challenge, but we observe that prior work combining QAT with KD suffers from a fundamental limitation: during distillation, the range mismatch between the teacher and the quantized student model induces an unattainable residual, resulting in an irreducible lower bound on the distillation loss. Motivated by this observation, we propose SQuaT (Student-Aware Quantized Teacher Features), a label-free QAT framework with KD that theoretically eliminates this lower bound by applying the student's quantization parameters to quantize the teacher's features during distillation. Through comprehensive experiments across diverse settings, we demonstrate that SQuaT consistently outperforms strong baselines, with particularly pronounced gains in extreme low-bit (e.g., 1- and 2-bit) settings. Furthermore, extensive evaluations across various model design choices show that our approach does not rely on specific architectural assumptions, making it broadly applicable across diverse architectures and quantization settings. The source code is available at https://github.com/lcdbsa522/SQuaT.

</details>


### [163] [Long-Time Trajectory Approximation via SA-NODEs: Model Predictive and Floquet Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10738)
*Ziqian Li,Nikolaos M. Matzakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究半自主神经ODE（SA-NODE）在长时间序列上逼近动力系统的误差问题，指出单一网络在整个时域上训练的误差界会随水平长度呈双指数恶化，并提出两种基于状态重置的训练策略（模型预测策略和Floquet策略）来规避这一障碍，分别通过自适应分区重启动和返回映射收缩保证误差随周期线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经常微分方程在长时间动态系统逼近中，单一网络在整个时域训练时其误差界随水平长度呈双指数恶化，这一屏障严重限制了SA-NODE在长时程预测中的可用性与可靠性，亟需新的训练策略来突破。

Method: 提出两种训练策略：(1) 模型预测策略——自适应划分水平区间，每个窗口从观测数据重启训练，当各窗口达到指定容差时，复合模型在时间上均匀满足容差，且参数预算随水平线性增长；(2) Floquet策略——针对具有稳定极限环的自洽目标，部署时无需数据，通过学习返回映射的认证收缩将误差限制为随周期数线性增长。同时给出时间周期架构下退化标量证书的轨道性保证及不可能性结果。

Result: 在四个基准数值实验中验证了预测的误差规律，并度量了每个保证的假设条件。模型预测策略实现参数预算线性增长且时间均匀容差，Floquet策略实现无部署数据下的误差线性增长，同时证明了周期性学习场中小区间误差与收缩频闪映射不能同时成立的障碍性结论。

Conclusion: SA-NODE的长时间逼近可通过适当的重置训练策略来避免双指数误差屏障，模型预测策略适用于一般目标，Floquet策略适用于周期极限环目标，两种策略均提供了可验证的理论保证和数值验证。

Abstract: We study the approximation of dynamical systems by semi-autonomous neural ordinary differential equations (SA-NODEs) over long time horizons. For a single network trained on the whole horizon, the available error bound deteriorates double exponentially in the horizon length. We develop two training strategies that avoid this barrier, each built on a reset of the state. The model predictive strategy partitions the horizon adaptively and restarts every window from observed data: when training meets a prescribed tolerance on every window, the composite model meets it uniformly in time, with a parameter budget linear in the horizon for targets with a bounded, uniformly regular reachable tube. The Floquet strategy addresses autonomous targets with a stable limit cycle and uses no data at deployment: a certified contraction of the learned return map confines the error to linear growth in the number of elapsed periods. For the time-periodic architecture we deploy, the scalar certificate degenerates; we prove instead a uniform-in-time orbital guarantee whose hypotheses are measured on the trained model, and an obstruction showing that, for an exactly periodic learned field, small one-period error and a contracting stroboscopic map cannot hold at once. Numerical experiments on four benchmarks confirm the predicted error laws and measure the hypotheses of every guarantee.

</details>


### [164] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于值函数的路径积分价值匹配（PI-VM）算法，通过截断和边缘化原始路径积分公式推导出值函数的时序递归形式，并利用时序差分学习和Girsanov定理实现离策略训练，以解决现有策略型LQ-SOC方法计算成本高和训练不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略型LQ-SOC方法（如基于策略梯度的算法）依赖全轨迹模拟，导致计算成本高昂且训练不稳定；虽然路径积分控制（PIC）被重新审视，但标准PIC同样面临高方差瓶颈，因此需要一种更高效、更稳定的值函数学习方法。

Method: 首先通过对原始路径积分公式进行截断和边缘化，推导出值函数的时序递归形式；然后基于该理论基础提出Path Integral Value Matching（PI-VM）算法，采用时序差分（TD）学习来逼近递归值动态，并结合Girsanov定理与经验回放（experience replay）实现离策略（off-policy）训练。

Result: 在多个随机最优控制（SOC）基准和采样任务上，PI-VM在低维场景中达到了与SOTA策略方法相当的精度，同时实现了数量级的效率提升；在高维场景中有效缓解了模式崩溃问题。

Conclusion: PI-VM为求解复杂SOC问题提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，证明了从策略方法转向值方法能够在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率，并增强高维场景下的稳定性。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [165] [MoE Proxy Models for Low-Cost Failure Reproduction and Diagnosis in LLM RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10823)
*Yikai Wang,Chuansai Zhou,Yuhang Zhou,Weiqiang Wu,Cong Wu,Yue Deng,Ben Feng,Mingming Zhu,Beirong Zhou,Zhibin Wang,Sheng Zhong,Chen Tian,Wangze Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了华为昇腾平台上大规模RL训练中的故障类型，识别出三类影响故障复现的模型侧因素，并提出一种基于结构保持和聚类的专家剪枝方法来构建代理模型，用于低成本故障复现与辅助诊断。实验表明代理模型可减少50%-87.5%的加速器需求和最多33.3倍的单步NPU-小时成本，同时保留主要训练动态并复现与原始模型一致的故障响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的强化学习后训练计算量大、系统管线复杂且调试开销高，在大模型上直接复现故障需要大量时间和计算资源，亟需一种低成本的故障调查与诊断方法。

Method: 提出一种代理模型构建方法，采用结构保持的、基于聚类的专家剪枝技术，选择代表性专家，同时保留模型的骨干架构、路由机制和基本任务能力，从而实现低成本的故障复现与辅助诊断。

Result: 代理模型将加速器需求降低50%-87.5%，每步NPU-小时成本最高降低33.3倍，同时保留主要训练动态并成功复现与原始模型一致的故障响应。

Conclusion: 代理模型可作为RL后训练中故障复现、定向验证和辅助诊断的低成本替代方案，有效解决大规模训练调试成本高昂的问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is computationally intensive and involves complex system pipelines with substantial debugging overhead. In practice, factors such as framework adaptation, numerical precision, and operator implementation can cause failures, including gradient overflow and loss divergence. Reproducing such failures directly on large models requires considerable time and computational resources. This paper systematically analyzes failures encountered during large-scale RL training on the Huawei Ascend platform, summarizes representative failure types, and identifies three model-side factors relevant to fault reproduction. Based on these factors, we propose a proxy-model construction method for low-cost fault investigation and auxiliary diagnosis. It employs structure-preserving, clustering-based expert pruning to select representative experts while retaining the model's backbone architecture, routing mechanism, and basic task capabilities. Our experimental results show that the proxy models reduce accelerator requirements by 50%-87.5% and achieve up to a 33.3x reduction in per-step NPU-hour cost, while preserving major training dynamics and reproducing fault responses consistent with the original models. Overall, the proxy models can serve as low-cost surrogates for fault reproduction, targeted validation, and auxiliary diagnosis in RL post-training.

</details>


### [166] [TACTICL: Task-Aware Compression of Tabular ICL Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10837)
*Mykhailo Koshil,Matthias Feurer,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TACTICL，一个面向表格上下文学习模型的任务感知自动压缩框架，通过联合剪枝Transformer层并替换为轻量级适配器，在保持上下文学习能力的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在表格任务上性能强劲但推理成本高昂，现有蒸馏方法虽能压缩模型却损失了上下文自适应能力。作者旨在实现模型压缩与上下文学习能力的兼得。

Method: TACTICL框架联合进行Transformer层剪枝和轻量适配器替换，通过下游任务训练实现任务感知的自动压缩，将上下文学习与权重学习相结合。

Result: 在47个基准数据集上验证，可替换高达85%的层而性能无明显下降，且对数据偏移保持鲁棒性，上下文能力完好保留。

Conclusion: TACTICL提供了一个稳健的框架，通过任务特定适应与结构化压缩相结合，有效利用表格基础模型在深度维度的冗余性，实现高效推理。

Abstract: The strong performance of foundation models for tabular tasks comes at substantial inference costs. Distilling models into task-specific architectures reduces model size and computational demands but also sacrifices in-context adaptability. Here we introduce TACTICL, an automated task-aware compression framework for tabular in-context learning models that jointly prunes transformer layers and replaces them with lightweight adapters trained on downstream tasks, thus blending in-context with in-weight learning. We study TACTICL on 47 benchmark datasets and show that we can substitute up to 85% of layers without substantial performance drop on a given downstream task. We further show that TACTICL maintains robustness to data shifts, leaving its in-context ability intact. Overall, TACTICL provides a robust framework for exploiting the depth-wise redundancy of tabular foundation models by combining task-specific adaptation and structured compression. We provide the code at: https://github.com/Hebog/tfm_compression

</details>


### [167] [Diffract: Spectral View of LLM Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10850)
*Nikita Borodin,Maria Krylova,Artem Zabolotnyi,Dmitry Aspisov,Egor Shikov,Nikita Tyuplyaev,Oleg Travkin,Roman Alferov,Dmitry Vinichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了持续预训练（CPT）如何将通用大语言模型适应到数学、指令、代码和自然文本等专业领域。通过奇异值分解分析，发现CPT主要改变奇异向量而非奇异值谱；注意力头投影矩阵表现出强烈的领域依赖异质性，据此定义头重要性准则，可移除高达60%的头更新而不损失质量，且选择性回退低重要性头可提升基准准确率最多4%。此外发现领域连通性——CPT检查点之间的线性插值可实现平滑的领域质量插值。论文还发布了Diffract开源工具包，用于十亿参数模型的规模化谱分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在适应专业领域时，其内部表示和权重矩阵究竟如何变化尚不清楚。作者希望通过理解CPT期间模型权重的底层结构和注意力头的角色，找到更高效、更精准的领域适应方法，避免无差别地更新所有参数。

Method: 使用奇异值分解（SVD）分析权重矩阵在CPT前后的低频奇异值谱变化；对注意力头投影矩阵进行逐头分析，定义头重要性准则用于识别哪些头的更新可以省略或回退；通过检查点间线性插值验证领域连通性；开发Diffract工具包实现十亿参数模型的规模化谱分析。

Result: CPT不显著改变奇异值谱，适应主要由奇异向量变化驱动；最多60%的头更新可以被移除而不损失质量；选择性回退低重要性头比完全训练基线提升最多4%的基准准确率；CPT检查点间线性插值在多个领域上均保持平滑的质量表现。

Conclusion: 持续预训练领域适应的核心机制是对奇异向量和注意力头的精细重排，而非对权重幅值的整体调整。选择性回退无关头的策略提供了一种更高效、更精准的领域适应方案，且不同领域CPT之间的连通性预示着可以共享或组合多个领域的适应成果。

Abstract: We study continual pre-training (CPT) as a mechanism for adapting general-purpose large language models to specialized domains: mathematics, instruction, code, and natural text. Using singular value decomposition of weight matrices, we find that CPT leaves singular value spectra largely invariant, with adaptation driven mainly by changes in singular vectors. An analysis of attention-head projection matrices reveals strong, domain-dependent head heterogeneity, which we exploit to define a head importance criterion: up to 60% of head updates can be removed without measurable quality loss. Selectively rewinding low-importance heads to their pre-trained state improves benchmark accuracy by up to 4% versus the fully trained baseline. Finally, we identify domain connectivity - linear interpolation between CPT checkpoints yields smooth domain-quality interpolation without notable degradation on either domain - and release Diffract, an open-source toolkit for scalable spectral analysis of billion-parameter models.

</details>


### [168] [FiGuRO: Intrinsic Dimension Estimation for Multi-Modal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10857)
*Viktoria Schuster,Sana Tonekaboni,Caroline Uhler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fidelity-Guided Rank Optimization (FiGuRO)框架，用于在模型容量和超参数约束下估计单模态和多模态数据的本征维度（ID），通过截断奇异值分解学习低秩投影维度，并实现了共享与私有信息的解耦，无需复杂辅助损失函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定数据的本征维度（ID）对于高效可解释的表征学习至关重要，但在多模态场景中解耦共享和私有信息尤为困难。现有方法存在关键空白：通常是静态的、单模态的，对比学习方法只能隐式适应共享ID，无法显式估计ID。

Method: FiGuRO使用截断奇异值分解（truncated SVD）学习低秩投影的维度，并通过算法决定何时在哪个潜在空间中增加或减少维度。共享与私有信息的解耦作为该优化过程的涌现属性自然产生，无需复杂的辅助损失函数。

Result: FiGuRO优于现有ID估计技术，对超参数变化更鲁棒。在模拟和真实数据上，FiGuRO能够捕获不同ID尺度和变化的子空间比例，成功分解共享和私有信息。此外，可应用于现代单模态预训练模型，实现高效的后处理多模态表征解耦。

Conclusion: FiGuRO为多模态和单模态数据的本征维度估计提供了一种鲁棒且高效的框架，其解耦能力作为优化副产品自然涌现，并可推广到预训练模型的后处理应用中。

Abstract: Determining the complexity, or Intrinsic Dimension (ID), of data is fundamental to efficient and interpretable representation learning. This is particularly challenging in multi-modal settings when trying to learn disentangled representations for shared and private information. Existing techniques leave a critical gap: they are often static, uni-modal, or in the case of contrastive methods, adapt only to the shared ID implicitly. We introduce Fidelity-Guided Rank Optimization (FiGuRO), a framework for approximating the ID of uni- and multi-modal data under constraints of model capacity and hyperparameters. FiGuRO learns the dimensions of low-rank projections using truncated singular value decomposition and an algorithm that determines when to reduce or increase dimension and in which latent space. Disentanglement of shared and private information arises as an emergent property of this optimization, eliminating the need for complex auxiliary loss functions. We demonstrate that FiGuRO outperforms existing ID estimation techniques and is more robust to hyperparameter changes. Across simulations and real-world data, FiGuRO captures distinct ID scales and varying subspace ratios, and decomposes shared and private information successfully. Furthermore, we show that FiGuRO can be applied to modern uni-modal pretrained models, enabling efficient, post-hoc disentanglement of multi-modal representations.

</details>


### [169] [Can Bayesian Optimization Efficiently Find a Strong Single Expert in Neural Thickets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10867)
*Nigel Bastian Cendra,Abdelhamid Ezzerg,Fernando Julio Cendra,Jeremias Knoblauch,Jakob Zeitler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯优化的无梯度后训练方法，在随机线性嵌入的低维子空间中进行结构化搜索，以显著降低大语言模型后训练的评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无梯度后训练方法虽然避免了梯度计算，但评估成本仍然很高。作者受到"有用的权重更新位于低维子空间"这一证据的启发，希望用更少的评估预算找到更优的单一专家模型。

Method: 在权重空间的随机线性嵌入（低维子空间）中应用贝叶斯优化，使用高斯过程代理模型来引导候选解的评估，整个过程不需要反向传播。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，使用Qwen2.5-Instruct（0.5B到3B参数规模），贝叶斯优化仅用RandOpt五分之一的候选评估次数即可匹配或超越其性能。

Conclusion: 代理引导的结构化搜索能够大幅降低无梯度后训练的评估成本，同时产生更强的可部署单一专家模型。

Abstract: Gradient-free post-training has emerged as a compelling alternative to gradient-based optimization for large language models (LLMs), but existing approaches remain costly. We ask whether structured search can identify a strong single expert under a modest evaluation budget. Motivated by evidence that useful weight updates lie in low-dimensional subspaces, we apply Bayesian optimization within a random linear embedding of weight space. Our method requires no backpropagation and uses a Gaussian process surrogate to guide candidate evaluations efficiently. Across several reasoning benchmarks with Qwen2.5-Instruct models from 0.5B to 3B parameters, Bayesian optimization using five times less candidate evaluations matches or exceeds RandOpt. These results show that surrogate-guided search can substantially reduce the evaluation cost of gradient-free post-training while producing stronger deployable single experts.

</details>


### [170] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了多类学习乐观超额风险界在可实现与不可知两个端点之间的空白：对于任意固定oracle风险L*，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n) + d_DS/n)，并推广到列表学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的多类学习最坏情况界在最优分类器已接近正确时不会变小，缺少随oracle风险本身波动的乐观率保证。最优超额风险在两个端点已知（可实现d_DS/n，不可知√(d_N/n)+d_DS/n），但中间区间是开放的。

Method: 上界：在[CEH+26]的cover-menu-compression架构和[Pab26]的可实现速率基础上，提出新的面向比较器的相对压缩定理：一个大小为k且经验上优于比较器h的压缩规则，其总体风险至多为L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)，无需稳定性。下界：通过校准到L*的pair-Assouad方案和伪立方体上的fiber论证，利用一个类别和一个分布在每个固定L*处同时强制两个项。

Result: 在每一固定oracle风险L*处，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，对字母表大小一致。两个定理均推广到列表学习：针对最优r元组假设，获得相同形状的乐观率和下界，移除了已知可实现列表下界中的因子r。

Conclusion: 通过组合相对压缩定理与pair-Assouad下界方案，完整刻画了多类学习从可实现到不可知之间的全部超额风险谱，并将结果一致推广到列表学习场景。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [171] [Benchmarking Time Series Generation Methods for Privacy-Preserving Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10891)
*Luis Amorim,Vitor Cerqueira,Moises Santos,Paulo J. Azevedo,Carlos Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TSTR协议下的基准测试，系统评估了合成时间序列生成方法在完全替代原始数据训练时的预测性能与隐私风险，并提出了隐私驱动的生成器Grasynda-P。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私敏感领域的时间序列预测需要基于发布数据而非原始观测进行训练，但合成时间序列生成方法在完全替代原始数据时的表现以及所携带的隐私风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 建立涵盖七个数据集的基准测试，采用Train on Synthetic, Test on Real (TSTR)协议，联合评估预测性能和基于距离的经验隐私风险；并引入Grasynda-P，即基于图的生成器Grasynda的隐私驱动扩展版本，采用矩阵集成和核密度估计技术。

Result: (1)没有任何生成方法能完全替代原始训练数据；(2)基于噪声的匿名化方法隐私保护最强但预测性能最差；(3)简单基于变换的生成器在预测任务上优于深度生成模型；(4)Grasynda-P位于Pareto前沿，在取得具有竞争力的预测性能的同时，比其他生成器具有更强的隐私分离度。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为评估和开发新的隐私感知合成时间序列生成方法建立了参考点，揭示了预测性能与隐私保护之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Time series forecasting in privacy-sensitive domains often requires training models on released data rather than original observations. Synthetic time series generation has been developed primarily for data augmentation, where generated series supplement the original training set. How well these methods perform when fully replacing the original data - and how much privacy risk the released series carry - remains underexplored. We address this gap through a benchmark evaluating synthetic generation methods and noise-based anonymization baselines under a Train on Synthetic, Test on Real (TSTR) protocol. We jointly assess forecasting performance and distance-based empirical privacy risk across seven datasets, characterizing the trade-off between these objectives. We also introduce Grasynda-P, a privacy-motivated extension of the graph-based generator Grasynda, incorporating matrix ensembling and kernel density estimation. Our results show that: (1) no generation method fully substitutes for original training data; (2) noise-based anonymization yields the strongest privacy but the worst forecasting performance; (3) simple transformation-based generators outperform deep generative models for forecasting in this setting; and (4) Grasynda-P lies on the Pareto frontier, achieving competitive forecasting with stronger privacy separation than other generators. This benchmark establishes a reference point for evaluating and developing new privacy-aware synthetic time series generation methods.

</details>


### [172] [Partially Observable Learning for Multi-Platform Dispatch Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10897)
*Fengming Yao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POLO，一个面向多平台即时配送系统的部分可观测多智能体强化学习框架，通过平台-网格独立智能体建模、注意力机制策略表示和反事实奖励塑形，在部分可观测条件下实现高效的配送调度优化，在收益和骑手效率上均超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的即时配送平台面临骑手多平台共享导致的部分可观测性问题，而现有调度优化工作大多假设完全可观测性和强制接单，在多平台真实场景下性能严重下降。

Method: POLO框架包含三个核心组件：1) 将每个平台-网格对建模为独立智能体，仅基于平台本地观测学习调度策略；2) 提出基于注意力的策略表示，选择性聚合异构骑手信息以应对不完整观测；3) 设计反事实奖励塑形机制，缓解跨网格联合行动导致的非平稳性问题，提升学习稳定性与可扩展性。

Result: 在高保真模拟器上的大量实验表明，POLO在不同平台数量和系统规模下均稳定优于强基线方法，在平台收益和骑手出行效率两个指标上表现突出，验证了其在多平台部分可观测场景下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: POLO通过将现实约束纳入强化学习框架设计，有效解决了多平台即时配送中的部分可观测调度问题，为真实场景下的大规模配送优化提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Instant delivery platforms have become a critical component of urban logistics, increasingly relying on crowdsourced couriers to fulfill highly dynamic orders. In real-world systems, couriers are not exclusive to a single platform and may concurrently serve multiple platforms, while each platform can only observe its own orders and couriers' interactions due to privacy and operational constraints. This results in a multi-platform dispatch environment with inherent partial observability. However, most existing works on dispatch optimization assume full courier observability and mandatory assignment acceptance, causing substantial performance degradation when deployed in realistic multi-platform settings. In this paper, we propose POLO, a partially observable multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for dispatching optimization in multi-platform instant delivery systems.
